**本章主线：** 市场与资金 → 购买支付 → 收支与指标 → 企业交易 → 财富报酬 → 求职经营与治理

**最后尝试回答：** 讲清一家公司的活动：投入、产品、交易、收入成本、招聘与风险。

### 词群 16.01｜经济活动如何组织并获得资金 {#group-16-01}

**串联方式：** 从经济、贸易、市场和产业进入商人、金融、资金及捐赠，再用广告和口号推广。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-001">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-001" class="vocabulary-word-title">economy<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-001" aria-label="定位 economy">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-001" data-mark-label="economy" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="economy：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="economy：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=economy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 economy" title="在有道查询 economy">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-001</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.经济；节约</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The local <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=economy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>economy</strong></a> depends on trade, a stable market and the tourism industry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">当地经济依赖贸易、稳定的市场和旅游业。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A strong <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=economy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>economy</strong></a> can support trade while a competitive market encourages industry to innovate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">强劲的经济能支撑贸易，而竞争性市场鼓励产业创新。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When trade slows, the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=economy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>economy</strong></a> suffers, the job market weakens and industry cuts investment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">贸易放缓时，经济受损，就业市场走弱，产业削减投资。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a growing economy</span> · 增长中的经济</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investment</a></span> · 投资</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=employment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">employment</a></span> · 就业</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-002">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-002" class="vocabulary-word-title">trade<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-002" aria-label="定位 trade">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-002" data-mark-label="trade" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="trade：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="trade：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 trade" title="在有道查询 trade">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-002</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.贸易；v.交换</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The local economy depends on <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trade</strong></a>, a stable market and the tourism industry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">当地经济依赖贸易、稳定的市场和旅游业。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A strong economy can support <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trade</strong></a> while a competitive market encourages industry to innovate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">强劲的经济能支撑贸易，而竞争性市场鼓励产业创新。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trade</strong></a> slows, the economy suffers, the job market weakens and industry cuts investment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">贸易放缓时，经济受损，就业市场走弱，产业削减投资。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">international trade</span> · 国际贸易</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investment</a></span> · 投资</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=employment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">employment</a></span> · 就业</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-003">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-003" class="vocabulary-word-title">market<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-003" aria-label="定位 market">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-003" data-mark-label="market" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="market：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="market：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=market&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 market" title="在有道查询 market">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-003</span></span></summary>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The local economy depends on trade, a stable <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=market&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>market</strong></a> and the tourism industry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">当地经济依赖贸易、稳定的市场和旅游业。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A strong economy can support trade while a competitive <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=market&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>market</strong></a> encourages industry to innovate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">强劲的经济能支撑贸易，而竞争性市场鼓励产业创新。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When trade slows, the economy suffers, the job <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=market&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>market</strong></a> weakens and industry cuts investment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">贸易放缓时，经济受损，就业市场走弱，产业削减投资。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-005">

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.商人；adj.商业的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A wealthy <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=merchant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>merchant</strong></a> offered financial support to the village relief fund.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位富商为村里的救济基金提供资金支持。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=merchant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>merchant</strong></a> sought financial advice before contributing to the education fund.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">商人在向教育基金捐款前寻求了财务建议。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Financial records show that the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=merchant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>merchant</strong></a> helped establish a fund for apprentices.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">财务记录显示，这位商人帮助设立了学徒基金。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a relief fund</span> · 救济基金</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=donation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">donation</a></span> · 捐款</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=apprentice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">apprentice</a></span> · 学徒</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-008">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-008" class="vocabulary-word-title">donate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-008" aria-label="定位 donate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-008" data-mark-label="donate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="donate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="donate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=donate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 donate" title="在有道查询 donate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-008</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Alumni can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=donate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>donate</strong></a> equipment or endow a scholarship for disadvantaged students.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">校友可以捐赠设备，或捐资设立帮助贫困学生的奖学金。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=donate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>donate</strong></a> a single painting, she decided to endow an entire art programme.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她决定捐资支持整个艺术项目，而非仅捐一幅画。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The appeal invites businesses to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=donate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>donate</strong></a> books and wealthy individuals to endow research posts.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这项募捐呼吁企业捐书，并邀请富裕人士捐资设立研究职位。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">donate money</span> · 捐款</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=alumni&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">alumni</a></span> · 校友</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=philanthropy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">philanthropy</a></span> · 慈善</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Alumni can donate equipment or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=endow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>endow</strong></a> a scholarship for disadvantaged students.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">校友可以捐赠设备，或捐资设立帮助贫困学生的奖学金。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than donate a single painting, she decided to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=endow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>endow</strong></a> an entire art programme.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她决定捐资支持整个艺术项目，而非仅捐一幅画。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The appeal invites businesses to donate books and wealthy individuals to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=endow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>endow</strong></a> research posts.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这项募捐呼吁企业捐书，并邀请富裕人士捐资设立研究职位。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=alumni&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">alumni</a></span> · 校友</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=philanthropy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">philanthropy</a></span> · 慈善</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-010">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-010" class="vocabulary-word-title">commercial<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-010" aria-label="定位 commercial">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-010" data-mark-label="commercial" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="commercial：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="commercial：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commercial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 commercial" title="在有道查询 commercial">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-010</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.商业的；n.商业广告</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commercial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>commercial</strong></a> team will advertise the service with a memorable slogan.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">商务团队将用一句令人难忘的口号宣传这项服务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before we advertise, we need a slogan that reflects our <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commercial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>commercial</strong></a> values.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">做广告之前，我们需要一句体现商业价值观的口号。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Schools should question any <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commercial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>commercial</strong></a> campaign that uses a slogan to advertise unhealthy food.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学校应质疑任何用口号宣传不健康食品的商业活动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">commercial advertising</span> · 商业广告</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=brand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">brand</a></span> · 品牌</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consumer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consumer</a></span> · 消费者</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-011">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-011" class="vocabulary-word-title">advertise<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-011" aria-label="定位 advertise">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-011" data-mark-label="advertise" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="advertise：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="advertise：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=advertise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 advertise" title="在有道查询 advertise">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-011</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.做广告</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The commercial team will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=advertise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>advertise</strong></a> the service with a memorable slogan.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">商务团队将用一句令人难忘的口号宣传这项服务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before we <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=advertise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>advertise</strong></a>, we need a slogan that reflects our commercial values.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">做广告之前，我们需要一句体现商业价值观的口号。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Schools should question any commercial campaign that uses a slogan to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=advertise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>advertise</strong></a> unhealthy food.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学校应质疑任何用口号宣传不健康食品的商业活动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">advertise a product</span> · 为产品做广告</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=brand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">brand</a></span> · 品牌</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consumer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consumer</a></span> · 消费者</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 16.02｜消费者怎样购买与租用 {#group-16-02}

**串联方式：** 先判断负担能力，再比较零售、连锁与杂货店，使用折扣、促销和优惠券。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-013">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-013" class="vocabulary-word-title">purchase<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-013" aria-label="定位 purchase">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-013" data-mark-label="purchase" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="purchase：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="purchase：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=purchase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 purchase" title="在有道查询 purchase">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-013</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.购买；v.购买</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We cannot afford to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=purchase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>purchase</strong></a> the building, so we will negotiate a lease.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们买不起这栋楼，因此将协商租约。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A long lease lets the charity afford better equipment without a costly property <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=purchase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>purchase</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">长期租约让慈善机构无须花巨资买房，便能负担更好的设备。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If you can afford the monthly lease, delaying the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=purchase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>purchase</strong></a> may preserve your savings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果你付得起每月租金，推迟购买可能保住你的积蓄。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">purchase equipment</span> · 购买设备</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rent</a></span> · 租金</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ownership&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ownership</a></span> · 所有权</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-014">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-014" class="vocabulary-word-title">lease<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-014" aria-label="定位 lease">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-014" data-mark-label="lease" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="lease：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="lease：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lease&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 lease" title="在有道查询 lease">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-014</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.租约；v.出租；租用</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We cannot afford to purchase the building, so we will negotiate a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lease&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lease</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们买不起这栋楼，因此将协商租约。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A long <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lease&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lease</strong></a> lets the charity afford better equipment without a costly property purchase.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">长期租约让慈善机构无须花巨资买房，便能负担更好的设备。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If you can afford the monthly <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lease&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lease</strong></a>, delaying the purchase may preserve your savings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果你付得起每月租金，推迟购买可能保住你的积蓄。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">sign a lease</span> · 签租约</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-015">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-015" class="vocabulary-word-title">afford<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-015" aria-label="定位 afford">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-015" data-mark-label="afford" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="afford：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="afford：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=afford&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 afford" title="在有道查询 afford">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-015</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.负担得起；提供，给予</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We cannot <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=afford&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>afford</strong></a> to purchase the building, so we will negotiate a lease.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们买不起这栋楼，因此将协商租约。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A long lease lets the charity <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=afford&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>afford</strong></a> better equipment without a costly property purchase.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">长期租约让慈善机构无须花巨资买房，便能负担更好的设备。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If you can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=afford&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>afford</strong></a> the monthly lease, delaying the purchase may preserve your savings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果你付得起每月租金，推迟购买可能保住你的积蓄。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">afford the rent</span> · 付得起租金</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rent</a></span> · 租金</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ownership&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ownership</a></span> · 所有权</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-016">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-016" class="vocabulary-word-title">retail<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-016" aria-label="定位 retail">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-016" data-mark-label="retail" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="retail：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="retail：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 retail" title="在有道查询 retail">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-016</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.零售；v.零售；以......价格零售</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The grocery chain keeps <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retail</strong></a> prices low by buying in bulk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这家连锁杂货店通过批量采购维持低零售价。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A new grocery chain has increased competition in the local <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retail</strong></a> sector.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新连锁杂货店加剧了当地零售业的竞争。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike the large chain, our grocery store relies on personal service to attract <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retail</strong></a> customers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与大型连锁店不同，我们的杂货店靠贴心服务吸引零售顾客。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">retail prices</span> · 零售价</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bulk%20buying&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bulk buying</a></span> · 批量采购</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=customer%20service&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">customer service</a></span> · 客户服务</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-017">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.连锁店；一连串(人或事物)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The grocery <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chain</strong></a> keeps retail prices low by buying in bulk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这家连锁杂货店通过批量采购维持低零售价。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A new grocery <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chain</strong></a> has increased competition in the local retail sector.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新连锁杂货店加剧了当地零售业的竞争。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike the large <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chain</strong></a>, our grocery store relies on personal service to attract retail customers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与大型连锁店不同，我们的杂货店靠贴心服务吸引零售顾客。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a supermarket chain</span> · 连锁超市</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bulk%20buying&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bulk buying</a></span> · 批量采购</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=customer%20service&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">customer service</a></span> · 客户服务</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=grocery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>grocery</strong></a> chain keeps retail prices low by buying in bulk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这家连锁杂货店通过批量采购维持低零售价。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=grocery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>grocery</strong></a> chain has increased competition in the local retail sector.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新连锁杂货店加剧了当地零售业的竞争。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike the large chain, our <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=grocery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>grocery</strong></a> store relies on personal service to attract retail customers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与大型连锁店不同，我们的杂货店靠贴心服务吸引零售顾客。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-019">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-019" class="vocabulary-word-title">discount<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-019" aria-label="定位 discount">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-019" data-mark-label="discount" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="discount：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="discount：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=discount&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 discount" title="在有道查询 discount">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-019</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 折扣；v. 打折出售</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the promotion, shoppers can use a voucher or coupon to obtain a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=discount&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>discount</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">促销期间，顾客可使用代金券或优惠券获得折扣。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The promotion offers a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=discount&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>discount</strong></a>, but its terms prohibit combining a voucher with a coupon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这次促销提供折扣，但条款禁止代金券和优惠券叠加。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Check whether the promotion requires a coupon before exchanging your voucher for a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=discount&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>discount</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用代金券享受折扣前，请确认该促销是否还要求优惠券。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a student discount</span> · 学生折扣</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=checkout&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">checkout</a></span> · 结账处</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terms%20and%20conditions&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">terms and conditions</a></span> · 条款</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.促销活动；晋升</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=promotion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>promotion</strong></a>, shoppers can use a voucher or coupon to obtain a discount.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">促销期间，顾客可使用代金券或优惠券获得折扣。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=promotion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>promotion</strong></a> offers a discount, but its terms prohibit combining a voucher with a coupon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这次促销提供折扣，但条款禁止代金券和优惠券叠加。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Check whether the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=promotion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>promotion</strong></a> requires a coupon before exchanging your voucher for a discount.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用代金券享受折扣前，请确认该促销是否还要求优惠券。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a sales promotion</span> · 促销活动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=checkout&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">checkout</a></span> · 结账处</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terms%20and%20conditions&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">terms and conditions</a></span> · 条款</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The promotion offers a discount, but its terms prohibit combining a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=voucher&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>voucher</strong></a> with a coupon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这次促销提供折扣，但条款禁止代金券和优惠券叠加。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Check whether the promotion requires a coupon before exchanging your <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=voucher&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>voucher</strong></a> for a discount.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用代金券享受折扣前，请确认该促销是否还要求优惠券。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-022">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-022" class="vocabulary-word-title">coupon<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-022" aria-label="定位 coupon">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-022" data-mark-label="coupon" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="coupon：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="coupon：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coupon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 coupon" title="在有道查询 coupon">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-022</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.优惠券</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the promotion, shoppers can use a voucher or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coupon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>coupon</strong></a> to obtain a discount.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">促销期间，顾客可使用代金券或优惠券获得折扣。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The promotion offers a discount, but its terms prohibit combining a voucher with a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coupon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>coupon</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这次促销提供折扣，但条款禁止代金券和优惠券叠加。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Check whether the promotion requires a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coupon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>coupon</strong></a> before exchanging your voucher for a discount.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用代金券享受折扣前，请确认该促销是否还要求优惠券。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a discount coupon</span> · 优惠券</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=checkout&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">checkout</a></span> · 结账处</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terms%20and%20conditions&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">terms and conditions</a></span> · 条款</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 16.03｜支付、凭证与税费 {#group-16-03}

**串联方式：** 先兑换货币，再分收据和发票，最后讨论税、关税及征收。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-023">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-023" class="vocabulary-word-title">currency<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-023" aria-label="定位 currency">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-023" data-mark-label="currency" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="currency：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="currency：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=currency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 currency" title="在有道查询 currency">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-023</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.货币；通用，流行</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students can swap foreign <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=currency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>currency</strong></a> at the exchange desk before travelling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生出发前可以在兑换柜台交换外币。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Banks charge for <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=currency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>currency</strong></a> exchange, so travellers sometimes swap leftover notes with friends.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">银行收取货币兑换费，因此旅客有时与朋友交换剩余纸币。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=currency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>currency</strong></a> exchange service lets customers swap euros for pounds at an agreed rate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">货币兑换服务让顾客按约定汇率将欧元换成英镑。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">foreign currency</span> · 外币</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exchange%20rate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">exchange rate</a></span> · 汇率</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=banknote&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">banknote</a></span> · 纸币</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-024">

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-025">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-025" class="vocabulary-word-title">swap<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-025" aria-label="定位 swap">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-025" data-mark-label="swap" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="swap：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="swap：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 swap" title="在有道查询 swap">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-025</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.交换(= swop )；n. 交换物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swap</strong></a> foreign currency at the exchange desk before travelling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生出发前可以在兑换柜台交换外币。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Banks charge for currency exchange, so travellers sometimes <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swap</strong></a> leftover notes with friends.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">银行收取货币兑换费，因此旅客有时与朋友交换剩余纸币。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A currency exchange service lets customers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swap</strong></a> euros for pounds at an agreed rate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">货币兑换服务让顾客按约定汇率将欧元换成英镑。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-026">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-026" class="vocabulary-word-title">receipt<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-026" aria-label="定位 receipt">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-026" data-mark-label="receipt" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="receipt：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="receipt：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=receipt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 receipt" title="在有道查询 receipt">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-026</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.收据；收到</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The invoice requests payment, while the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=receipt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>receipt</strong></a> confirms that payment was received.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">发票要求付款，而收据确认已经收到款项。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Keep both the invoice and the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=receipt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>receipt</strong></a> when claiming business expenses.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报销业务费用时，请同时保留发票和收据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The accountant matched each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=receipt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>receipt</strong></a> to its invoice before closing the accounts.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">会计结账前，把每张收据与对应发票核对。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">keep a receipt</span> · 保留收据</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=payment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">payment</a></span> · 付款</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accountant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accountant</a></span> · 会计</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-027">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-027" class="vocabulary-word-title">invoice<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-027" aria-label="定位 invoice">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-027" data-mark-label="invoice" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="invoice：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="invoice：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invoice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 invoice" title="在有道查询 invoice">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-027</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.发票；v.开发票给</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invoice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>invoice</strong></a> requests payment, while the receipt confirms that payment was received.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">发票要求付款，而收据确认已经收到款项。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Keep both the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invoice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>invoice</strong></a> and the receipt when claiming business expenses.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报销业务费用时，请同时保留发票和收据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The accountant matched each receipt to its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invoice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>invoice</strong></a> before closing the accounts.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">会计结账前，把每张收据与对应发票核对。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">issue an invoice</span> · 开具发票</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=payment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">payment</a></span> · 付款</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accountant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accountant</a></span> · 会计</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-028">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-028" class="vocabulary-word-title">tax<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-028" aria-label="定位 tax">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-028" data-mark-label="tax" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tax：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tax：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tax&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tax" title="在有道查询 tax">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-028</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.税；v.对......征税</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials may impose a new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tax&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tax</strong></a>, a pollution levy, an import tariff or customs duty.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员可能征收新税、污染税、进口关税或海关税。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before they impose a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tax&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tax</strong></a> or levy, ministers must explain how it differs from a tariff or duty.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">部长在征收税款前，须解释它与关税或海关税的区别。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The proposal would impose a carbon <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tax&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tax</strong></a> and a fuel levy while reducing the import tariff and customs duty.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该提案将征收碳税和燃油税，同时降低进口关税和海关税。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">income tax</span> · 所得税</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=taxpayer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">taxpayer</a></span> · 纳税人</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=public%20revenue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">public revenue</a></span> · 财政收入</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-029">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-029" class="vocabulary-word-title">levy<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-029" aria-label="定位 levy">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-029" data-mark-label="levy" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="levy：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="levy：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=levy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 levy" title="在有道查询 levy">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-029</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.税款；v.征收，征(税)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials may impose a new tax, a pollution <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=levy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>levy</strong></a>, an import tariff or customs duty.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员可能征收新税、污染税、进口关税或海关税。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before they impose a tax or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=levy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>levy</strong></a>, ministers must explain how it differs from a tariff or duty.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">部长在征收税款前，须解释它与关税或海关税的区别。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The proposal would impose a carbon tax and a fuel <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=levy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>levy</strong></a> while reducing the import tariff and customs duty.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该提案将征收碳税和燃油税，同时降低进口关税和海关税。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=taxpayer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">taxpayer</a></span> · 纳税人</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=public%20revenue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">public revenue</a></span> · 财政收入</li></ul>

### 词群 16.04｜核算消费、成本与收入 {#group-16-04}

**串联方式：** 区分消费、花费、成本、开支与营业收入；gross 可修饰未扣除成本税费的总额。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-033">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-033" class="vocabulary-word-title">consume<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-033" aria-label="定位 consume">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-033" data-mark-label="consume" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="consume：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="consume：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consume&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 consume" title="在有道查询 consume">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-033</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 消耗；吃，喝</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Households that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consume&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>consume</strong></a> less electricity can spend less without sacrificing comfort as energy costs affect the cost of living.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">少用电的家庭能在不牺牲舒适度的情况下少花钱，因为能源费用影响生活成本。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If factories <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consume&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>consume</strong></a> less fuel, they can spend more on wages and reduce the cost of production.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果工厂少消耗燃料，就能在工资上多投入，并降低生产成本。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Visitors <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consume&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>consume</strong></a> local food and spend money in shops, helping cover the cost of maintaining the market.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">游客消费当地食品并在商店花钱，帮助支付市场的维护成本。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">consume energy</span> · 消耗能源</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=household%20budget&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">household budget</a></span> · 家庭预算</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=energy%20efficiency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">energy efficiency</a></span> · 能源效率</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-034">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-034" class="vocabulary-word-title">spend<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-034" aria-label="定位 spend">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-034" data-mark-label="spend" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="spend：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="spend：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 spend" title="在有道查询 spend">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-034</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.花费；n.开支</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Households that consume less electricity can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spend</strong></a> less without sacrificing comfort as energy costs affect the cost of living.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">少用电的家庭能在不牺牲舒适度的情况下少花钱，因为能源费用影响生活成本。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If factories consume less fuel, they can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spend</strong></a> more on wages and reduce the cost of production.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果工厂少消耗燃料，就能在工资上多投入，并降低生产成本。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Visitors consume local food and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spend</strong></a> money in shops, helping cover the cost of maintaining the market.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">游客消费当地食品并在商店花钱，帮助支付市场的维护成本。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">spend money</span> · 花钱</p>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Gross revenue rose, but the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expense&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>expense</strong></a> of repairs absorbed most of the additional revenue.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">总收入增加了，但维修费用消耗了新增收入的大部分。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expense&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>expense</strong></a> report compares gross revenue with spending before tax.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">费用报告将总收入与税前支出进行比较。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A high gross revenue figure says little about profit unless every <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expense&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>expense</strong></a> is included.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">除非计入每项费用，否则很高的总收入数字并不能说明利润情况。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">business expense</span> · 业务开支</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=profit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">profit</a></span> · 利润</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=net%20income&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">net income</a></span> · 净收入</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-037">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Gross <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revenue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>revenue</strong></a> rose, but the expense of repairs absorbed most of the additional <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revenue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>revenue</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">总收入增加了，但维修费用消耗了新增收入的大部分。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The expense report compares gross <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revenue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>revenue</strong></a> with spending before tax.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">费用报告将总收入与税前支出进行比较。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A high gross <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revenue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>revenue</strong></a> figure says little about profit unless every expense is included.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">除非计入每项费用，否则很高的总收入数字并不能说明利润情况。</p></li>
