### 词群 16.05｜理解宏观风险与金融工具 {#group-16-05}

**串联方式：** 辨认衰退、萧条和危机，再分别学习证券债券、债务赤字与通胀通缩，不强行推导因果。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-039">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-039" class="vocabulary-word-title">recession<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-039" aria-label="定位 recession">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-039" data-mark-label="recession" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="recession：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="recession：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recession&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 recession" title="在有道查询 recession">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-039</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 经济衰退</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The financial crisis triggered a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recession&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recession</strong></a>, raising fears of another depression.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">金融危机引发了经济衰退，令人担忧会再次出现大萧条。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recession&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recession</strong></a> is generally less severe than a depression, although either can create a social crisis.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">经济衰退通常没有大萧条严重，但两者都可能造成社会危机。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the crisis, economists debated whether the downturn was a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recession&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recession</strong></a> or the start of a depression.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">危机期间，经济学家争论此次下滑是衰退还是萧条的开端。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an economic recession</span> · 经济衰退</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=downturn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">downturn</a></span> · 经济下行</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=unemployment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">unemployment</a></span> · 失业</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-040">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-040" class="vocabulary-word-title">depression<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-040" aria-label="定位 depression">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-040" data-mark-label="depression" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="depression：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="depression：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=depression&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 depression" title="在有道查询 depression">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-040</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.沮丧，消沉；萧条期，不景气</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The financial crisis triggered a recession, raising fears of another <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=depression&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>depression</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">金融危机引发了经济衰退，令人担忧会再次出现大萧条。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A recession is generally less severe than a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=depression&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>depression</strong></a>, although either can create a social crisis.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">经济衰退通常没有大萧条严重，但两者都可能造成社会危机。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the crisis, economists debated whether the downturn was a recession or the start of a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=depression&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>depression</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">危机期间，经济学家争论此次下滑是衰退还是萧条的开端。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the Great Depression</span> · 大萧条</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=downturn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">downturn</a></span> · 经济下行</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=unemployment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">unemployment</a></span> · 失业</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-041">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-041" class="vocabulary-word-title">crisis<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-041" aria-label="定位 crisis">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-041" data-mark-label="crisis" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="crisis：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="crisis：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crisis&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 crisis" title="在有道查询 crisis">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-041</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.危机</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The financial <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crisis&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crisis</strong></a> triggered a recession, raising fears of another depression.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">金融危机引发了经济衰退，令人担忧会再次出现大萧条。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A recession is generally less severe than a depression, although either can create a social <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crisis&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crisis</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">经济衰退通常没有大萧条严重，但两者都可能造成社会危机。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crisis&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crisis</strong></a>, economists debated whether the downturn was a recession or the start of a depression.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">危机期间，经济学家争论此次下滑是衰退还是萧条的开端。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a financial crisis</span> · 金融危机</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=downturn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">downturn</a></span> · 经济下行</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=unemployment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">unemployment</a></span> · 失业</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-042">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-042" class="vocabulary-word-title">security<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-042" aria-label="定位 security">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-042" data-mark-label="security" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="security：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="security：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=security&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 security" title="在有道查询 security">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-042</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.安全工作；安全；［securities］证券</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For greater financial <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=security&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>security</strong></a>, she invested part of her savings in a government bond.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为获得更稳妥的财务保障，她将部分积蓄投向政府债券。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A bond can provide regular income, but financial <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=security&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>security</strong></a> still requires attention to risk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">债券能提供定期收入，但财务保障仍要求关注风险。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The adviser discussed retirement <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=security&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>security</strong></a> before recommending a bond fund.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">顾问在推荐债券基金前讨论了退休财务保障。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">financial security</span> · 财务保障</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investment%20risk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investment risk</a></span> · 投资风险</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retirement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">retirement</a></span> · 退休</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-043">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-043" class="vocabulary-word-title">bond<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-043" aria-label="定位 bond">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-043" data-mark-label="bond" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bond：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bond：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bond&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bond" title="在有道查询 bond">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-043</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 债券</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For greater financial security, she invested part of her savings in a government <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bond&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bond</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为获得更稳妥的财务保障，她将部分积蓄投向政府债券。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bond&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bond</strong></a> can provide regular income, but financial security still requires attention to risk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">债券能提供定期收入，但财务保障仍要求关注风险。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The adviser discussed retirement security before recommending a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bond&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bond</strong></a> fund.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">顾问在推荐债券基金前讨论了退休财务保障。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a government bond</span> · 政府债券</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investment%20risk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investment risk</a></span> · 投资风险</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retirement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">retirement</a></span> · 退休</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-044">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-044" class="vocabulary-word-title">debt<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-044" aria-label="定位 debt">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-044" data-mark-label="debt" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="debt：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="debt：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=debt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 debt" title="在有道查询 debt">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-044</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.债务</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A persistent budget deficit can increase public <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=debt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>debt</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">持续的预算赤字可能增加公共债务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The government reduced its deficit but still owed a substantial <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=debt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>debt</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">政府缩小了赤字，但仍欠下巨额债务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Borrowing to cover the deficit adds to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=debt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>debt</strong></a> unless other liabilities fall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为弥补赤字而借款会增加债务，除非其他负债下降。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">public debt</span> · 公共债务</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=borrowing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">borrowing</a></span> · 借款</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fiscal%20policy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fiscal policy</a></span> · 财政政策</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-045">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-045" class="vocabulary-word-title">deficit<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-045" aria-label="定位 deficit">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-045" data-mark-label="deficit" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="deficit：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="deficit：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deficit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 deficit" title="在有道查询 deficit">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-045</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.赤字，逆差</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A persistent budget <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deficit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deficit</strong></a> can increase public debt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">持续的预算赤字可能增加公共债务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The government reduced its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deficit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deficit</strong></a> but still owed a substantial debt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">政府缩小了赤字，但仍欠下巨额债务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Borrowing to cover the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deficit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deficit</strong></a> adds to debt unless other liabilities fall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为弥补赤字而借款会增加债务，除非其他负债下降。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a budget deficit</span> · 预算赤字</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=borrowing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">borrowing</a></span> · 借款</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fiscal%20policy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fiscal policy</a></span> · 财政政策</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-046">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-046" class="vocabulary-word-title">inflation<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-046" aria-label="定位 inflation">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-046" data-mark-label="inflation" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="inflation：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="inflation：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inflation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 inflation" title="在有道查询 inflation">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-046</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.通货膨胀</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inflation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Inflation</strong></a> raises the general price level, whereas deflation lowers it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通胀推高总体物价水平，而通缩使其下降。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Policy makers worry about both rapid <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inflation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>inflation</strong></a> and prolonged deflation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">政策制定者既担心快速通胀，也担心长期通缩。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report compares household spending during periods of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inflation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>inflation</strong></a> and deflation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告比较了通胀和通缩时期的家庭支出。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">rising inflation</span> · 上升的通胀</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=price%20level&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">price level</a></span> · 物价水平</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=purchasing%20power&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">purchasing power</a></span> · 购买力</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-047">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-047" class="vocabulary-word-title">deflation<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-047" aria-label="定位 deflation">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-047" data-mark-label="deflation" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="deflation：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="deflation：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deflation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 deflation" title="在有道查询 deflation">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-047</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.通货紧缩</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inflation raises the general price level, whereas <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deflation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deflation</strong></a> lowers it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通胀推高总体物价水平，而通缩使其下降。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Policy makers worry about both rapid inflation and prolonged <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deflation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deflation</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">政策制定者既担心快速通胀，也担心长期通缩。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report compares household spending during periods of inflation and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deflation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deflation</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告比较了通胀和通缩时期的家庭支出。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">prolonged deflation</span> · 长期通缩</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=price%20level&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">price level</a></span> · 物价水平</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=purchasing%20power&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">purchasing power</a></span> · 购买力</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 16.06｜描述经济指标升降 {#group-16-06}

**串联方式：** 先记减少与下降，再看增长、进出口、股息、扩张与流入；各词搭配对象不同。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-048">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-048" class="vocabulary-word-title">dwindle<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-048" aria-label="定位 dwindle">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-048" data-mark-label="dwindle" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="dwindle：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="dwindle：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dwindle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 dwindle" title="在有道查询 dwindle">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-048</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 缩小，减少</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As orders <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dwindle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dwindle</strong></a> and demand shows a decrease, the factory may downsize.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">随着订单减少、需求下降，工厂可能缩减规模。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A decrease in funding forced the charity to downsize as its reserves continued to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dwindle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dwindle</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">资金减少迫使慈善机构缩减规模，其储备也继续萎缩。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The firm plans to downsize after a decrease in sales, but managers hope staff numbers will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dwindle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dwindle</strong></a> through retirement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">销售下降后，公司计划裁员，但经理希望通过退休自然减员。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">dwindle rapidly</span> · 迅速减少</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=redundancy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">redundancy</a></span> · 裁员</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reserves&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reserves</a></span> · 储备</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-049">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-049" class="vocabulary-word-title">decrease<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-049" aria-label="定位 decrease">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-049" data-mark-label="decrease" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="decrease：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="decrease：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decrease&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 decrease" title="在有道查询 decrease">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-049</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 减少，减小；v. (使)减少，(使)减小</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As orders dwindle and demand shows a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decrease&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>decrease</strong></a>, the factory may downsize.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">随着订单减少、需求下降，工厂可能缩减规模。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decrease&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>decrease</strong></a> in funding forced the charity to downsize as its reserves continued to dwindle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">资金减少迫使慈善机构缩减规模，其储备也继续萎缩。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The firm plans to downsize after a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decrease&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>decrease</strong></a> in sales, but managers hope staff numbers will dwindle through retirement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">销售下降后，公司计划裁员，但经理希望通过退休自然减员。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a decrease in demand</span> · 需求下降</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=redundancy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">redundancy</a></span> · 裁员</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reserves&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reserves</a></span> · 储备</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-050">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-050" class="vocabulary-word-title">downsize<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-050" aria-label="定位 downsize">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-050" data-mark-label="downsize" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="downsize：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="downsize：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=downsize&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 downsize" title="在有道查询 downsize">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-050</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.使精简；裁员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As orders dwindle and demand shows a decrease, the factory may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=downsize&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>downsize</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">随着订单减少、需求下降，工厂可能缩减规模。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A decrease in funding forced the charity to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=downsize&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>downsize</strong></a> as its reserves continued to dwindle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">资金减少迫使慈善机构缩减规模，其储备也继续萎缩。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The firm plans to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=downsize&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>downsize</strong></a> after a decrease in sales, but managers hope staff numbers will dwindle through retirement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">销售下降后，公司计划裁员，但经理希望通过退休自然减员。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">downsize the workforce</span> · 裁员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=redundancy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">redundancy</a></span> · 裁员</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reserves&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reserves</a></span> · 储备</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-051">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-051" class="vocabulary-word-title">decline<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-051" aria-label="定位 decline">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-051" data-mark-label="decline" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="decline：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="decline：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 decline" title="在有道查询 decline">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-051</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.下降；n.下降</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To reduce the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>decline</strong></a> in sales, we must ensure that delays do not diminish customer trust.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为减缓销量下降，我们必须确保延误不会削弱客户信任。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Better training may reduce errors and diminish the risk of a further <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>decline</strong></a> in quality.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">更好的培训可能减少错误，降低质量进一步下降的风险。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>decline</strong></a> in subsidies could diminish profits and force firms to reduce production.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">补贴下降可能削弱利润，迫使企业减产。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a decline in sales</span> · 销量下降</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=customer%20trust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">customer trust</a></span> · 客户信任</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=subsidy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">subsidy</a></span> · 补贴</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-052">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-052" class="vocabulary-word-title">diminish<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-052" aria-label="定位 diminish">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-052" data-mark-label="diminish" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="diminish：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="diminish：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diminish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 diminish" title="在有道查询 diminish">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-052</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. (使) 减少；贬低</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To reduce the decline in sales, we must ensure that delays do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diminish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>diminish</strong></a> customer trust.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为减缓销量下降，我们必须确保延误不会削弱客户信任。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Better training may reduce errors and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diminish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>diminish</strong></a> the risk of a further decline in quality.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">更好的培训可能减少错误，降低质量进一步下降的风险。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A decline in subsidies could <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diminish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>diminish</strong></a> profits and force firms to reduce production.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">补贴下降可能削弱利润，迫使企业减产。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">diminish demand</span> · 减少需求</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=customer%20trust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">customer trust</a></span> · 客户信任</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=subsidy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">subsidy</a></span> · 补贴</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-053">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-053" class="vocabulary-word-title">reduce<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-053" aria-label="定位 reduce">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-053" data-mark-label="reduce" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reduce：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reduce：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reduce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reduce" title="在有道查询 reduce">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-053</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.减少，减小</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reduce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reduce</strong></a> the decline in sales, we must ensure that delays do not diminish customer trust.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为减缓销量下降，我们必须确保延误不会削弱客户信任。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Better training may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reduce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reduce</strong></a> errors and diminish the risk of a further decline in quality.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">更好的培训可能减少错误，降低质量进一步下降的风险。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A decline in subsidies could diminish profits and force firms to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reduce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reduce</strong></a> production.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">补贴下降可能削弱利润，迫使企业减产。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reduce costs</span> · 降低成本</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=customer%20trust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">customer trust</a></span> · 客户信任</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=subsidy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">subsidy</a></span> · 补贴</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-054">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-054" class="vocabulary-word-title">increase<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-054" aria-label="定位 increase">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-054" data-mark-label="increase" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="increase：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="increase：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=increase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 increase" title="在有道查询 increase">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-054</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 增加，增长；v. (使)增加，(使)增长</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Economic growth can enrich communities if each wage increment produces a real <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=increase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>increase</strong></a> in income.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果每次加薪都带来实际收入增长，经济增长就能使社区富裕。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=increase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>increase</strong></a> in skills can enrich working lives and support growth beyond the annual pay increment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">技能提升能丰富职业生活，并带来超越年度加薪的成长。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Growth should enrich ordinary households, so an increment must at least match the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=increase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>increase</strong></a> in living costs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">增长应惠及普通家庭，因此加薪至少应跟上生活成本的上涨。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an increase in pay</span> · 加薪</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=living%20standards&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">living standards</a></span> · 生活水平</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=productivity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">productivity</a></span> · 生产率</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-055">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-055" class="vocabulary-word-title">increment<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-055" aria-label="定位 increment">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-055" data-mark-label="increment" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="increment：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="increment：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=increment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 increment" title="在有道查询 increment">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-055</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.增加量；定期的加薪</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Economic growth can enrich communities if each wage <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=increment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>increment</strong></a> produces a real increase in income.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果每次加薪都带来实际收入增长，经济增长就能使社区富裕。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An increase in skills can enrich working lives and support growth beyond the annual pay <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=increment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>increment</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">技能提升能丰富职业生活，并带来超越年度加薪的成长。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Growth should enrich ordinary households, so an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=increment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>increment</strong></a> must at least match the increase in living costs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">增长应惠及普通家庭，因此加薪至少应跟上生活成本的上涨。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an annual increment</span> · 年度加薪</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=living%20standards&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">living standards</a></span> · 生活水平</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=productivity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">productivity</a></span> · 生产率</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-056">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-056" class="vocabulary-word-title">growth<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-056" aria-label="定位 growth">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-056" data-mark-label="growth" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="growth：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="growth：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=growth&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 growth" title="在有道查询 growth">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-056</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.增长；生长</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Economic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=growth&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>growth</strong></a> can enrich communities if each wage increment produces a real increase in income.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果每次加薪都带来实际收入增长，经济增长就能使社区富裕。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An increase in skills can enrich working lives and support <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=growth&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>growth</strong></a> beyond the annual pay increment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">技能提升能丰富职业生活，并带来超越年度加薪的成长。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=growth&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Growth</strong></a> should enrich ordinary households, so an increment must at least match the increase in living costs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">增长应惠及普通家庭，因此加薪至少应跟上生活成本的上涨。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">economic growth</span> · 经济增长</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=living%20standards&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">living standards</a></span> · 生活水平</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=productivity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">productivity</a></span> · 生产率</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-057">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-057" class="vocabulary-word-title">enrich<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-057" aria-label="定位 enrich">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-057" data-mark-label="enrich" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="enrich：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="enrich：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enrich&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 enrich" title="在有道查询 enrich">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-057</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 使富有；充实</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Economic growth can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enrich&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enrich</strong></a> communities if each wage increment produces a real increase in income.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果每次加薪都带来实际收入增长，经济增长就能使社区富裕。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An increase in skills can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enrich&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enrich</strong></a> working lives and support growth beyond the annual pay increment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">技能提升能丰富职业生活，并带来超越年度加薪的成长。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Growth should <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enrich&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enrich</strong></a> ordinary households, so an increment must at least match the increase in living costs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">增长应惠及普通家庭，因此加薪至少应跟上生活成本的上涨。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">enrich communities</span> · 使社区富裕</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=living%20standards&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">living standards</a></span> · 生活水平</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=productivity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">productivity</a></span> · 生产率</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-058">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-058" class="vocabulary-word-title">export<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-058" aria-label="定位 export">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-058" data-mark-label="export" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="export：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="export：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=export&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 export" title="在有道查询 export">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-058</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 出口；n. 出口；出口商品</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Firms that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=export&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>export</strong></a> machinery and import fuel may pay a smaller dividend when energy prices rise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">出口机械、进口燃料的企业可能在能源涨价时少派股息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The company will import components, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=export&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>export</strong></a> finished goods and use profits to pay a dividend.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公司将进口零件、出口成品，并用利润派发股息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If we <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=export&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>export</strong></a> more than we import, the board expects to raise the dividend.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果出口额超过进口额，董事会预计将提高股息。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">export goods</span> · 出口商品</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shareholder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shareholder</a></span> · 股东</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=components&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">components</a></span> · 零部件</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-059">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-059" class="vocabulary-word-title">import<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-059" aria-label="定位 import">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-059" data-mark-label="import" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="import：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="import：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=import&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 import" title="在有道查询 import">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-059</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 进口；n. 进口；进口商品</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Firms that export machinery and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=import&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>import</strong></a> fuel may pay a smaller dividend when energy prices rise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">出口机械、进口燃料的企业可能在能源涨价时少派股息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The company will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=import&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>import</strong></a> components, export finished goods and use profits to pay a dividend.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公司将进口零件、出口成品，并用利润派发股息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If we export more than we <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=import&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>import</strong></a>, the board expects to raise the dividend.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果出口额超过进口额，董事会预计将提高股息。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">import fuel</span> · 进口燃料</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shareholder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shareholder</a></span> · 股东</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=components&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">components</a></span> · 零部件</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-060">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-060" class="vocabulary-word-title">dividend<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-060" aria-label="定位 dividend">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-060" data-mark-label="dividend" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="dividend：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="dividend：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dividend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 dividend" title="在有道查询 dividend">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-060</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 股息，红利</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Firms that export machinery and import fuel may pay a smaller <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dividend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dividend</strong></a> when energy prices rise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">出口机械、进口燃料的企业可能在能源涨价时少派股息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The company will import components, export finished goods and use profits to pay a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dividend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dividend</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公司将进口零件、出口成品，并用利润派发股息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If we export more than we import, the board expects to raise the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dividend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dividend</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果出口额超过进口额，董事会预计将提高股息。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">pay a dividend</span> · 派发股息</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shareholder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shareholder</a></span> · 股东</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=components&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">components</a></span> · 零部件</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-061">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-061" class="vocabulary-word-title">escalate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-061" aria-label="定位 escalate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-061" data-mark-label="escalate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="escalate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="escalate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=escalate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 escalate" title="在有道查询 escalate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-061</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. (使)逐步升级，(使)不断恶化</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An influx of investors can help firms expand, but competition for land may cause prices to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=escalate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>escalate</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">投资者涌入有助于企业扩张，但土地竞争可能导致价格上涨。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As costs <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=escalate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>escalate</strong></a>, the council fears that an influx of visitors will force hotels to expand too quickly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">成本不断上升，市政委员会担忧游客涌入会迫使酒店过快扩张。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We can expand services after the influx of funding, provided operating costs do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=escalate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>escalate</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">资金流入后我们可以扩大服务，前提是运营成本不会不断增加。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">escalate rapidly</span> · 迅速升级</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=operating%20costs&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">operating costs</a></span> · 运营成本</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investment</a></span> · 投资</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-062">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-062" class="vocabulary-word-title">expand<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-062" aria-label="定位 expand">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-062" data-mark-label="expand" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="expand：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="expand：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 expand" title="在有道查询 expand">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-062</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.扩充，扩大；扩展</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An influx of investors can help firms <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>expand</strong></a>, but competition for land may cause prices to escalate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">投资者涌入有助于企业扩张，但土地竞争可能导致价格上涨。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As costs escalate, the council fears that an influx of visitors will force hotels to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>expand</strong></a> too quickly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">成本不断上升，市政委员会担忧游客涌入会迫使酒店过快扩张。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>expand</strong></a> services after the influx of funding, provided operating costs do not escalate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">资金流入后我们可以扩大服务，前提是运营成本不会不断增加。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">expand operations</span> · 扩大业务</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=operating%20costs&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">operating costs</a></span> · 运营成本</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investment</a></span> · 投资</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-063">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-063" class="vocabulary-word-title">influx<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-063" aria-label="定位 influx">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-063" data-mark-label="influx" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="influx：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="influx：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=influx&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 influx" title="在有道查询 influx">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-063</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.流入，涌进</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=influx&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>influx</strong></a> of investors can help firms expand, but competition for land may cause prices to escalate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">投资者涌入有助于企业扩张，但土地竞争可能导致价格上涨。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As costs escalate, the council fears that an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=influx&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>influx</strong></a> of visitors will force hotels to expand too quickly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">成本不断上升，市政委员会担忧游客涌入会迫使酒店过快扩张。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We can expand services after the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=influx&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>influx</strong></a> of funding, provided operating costs do not escalate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">资金流入后我们可以扩大服务，前提是运营成本不会不断增加。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an influx of capital</span> · 资本流入</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=operating%20costs&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">operating costs</a></span> · 运营成本</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investment</a></span> · 投资</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 16.07｜资源是否足够 {#group-16-07}

**串联方式：** 从富裕、充足、富余和储蓄走向冗余与缺乏，练习数量是否足够的表达。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-064">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-064" class="vocabulary-word-title">affluent<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-064" aria-label="定位 affluent">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-064" data-mark-label="affluent" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="affluent：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="affluent：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=affluent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 affluent" title="在有道查询 affluent">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-064</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 富裕的，富足的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Even an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=affluent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>affluent</strong></a> city must provide sufficient funding for adequate housing.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">即使是富裕城市，也必须提供足够资金来保障住房。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=affluent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Affluent</strong></a> households usually have sufficient savings to secure adequate care in old age.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">富裕家庭通常有足够积蓄来保障老年生活照护。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A school need not be <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=affluent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>affluent</strong></a> to offer adequate facilities if it receives sufficient public support.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">只要获得足够公共支持，学校无须很富裕也能提供合格设施。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an affluent area</span> · 富裕地区</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inequality&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inequality</a></span> · 不平等</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=public%20services&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">public services</a></span> · 公共服务</li></ul>
</div>
</details>
