<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-067">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-067" class="vocabulary-word-title">spare<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-067" aria-label="定位 spare">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-067" data-mark-label="spare" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="spare：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="spare：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 spare" title="在有道查询 spare">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-067</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 多余的，空闲的；v. 剩余</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Selling redundant machines creates <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spare</strong></a> space and a saving where there is a lack of storage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">出售多余机器能腾出空闲空间，并在储存空间不足时节约费用。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A lack of demand left <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spare</strong></a> capacity, making some equipment redundant despite the energy saving it offered.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">需求不足造成产能闲置，一些设备虽能节能，却仍变得多余。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The office reused <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spare</strong></a> desks to address a lack of furniture, achieving a saving without buying redundant stock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">办公室利用闲置桌子解决家具不足，实现了节约，也避免买入多余库存。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">spare capacity</span> · 闲置产能</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=storage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">storage</a></span> · 储存</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=efficiency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">efficiency</a></span> · 效率</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-068">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-068" class="vocabulary-word-title">saving<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-068" aria-label="定位 saving">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-068" data-mark-label="saving" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="saving：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="saving：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=saving&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 saving" title="在有道查询 saving">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-068</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.节约，节省</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Selling redundant machines creates spare space and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=saving&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>saving</strong></a> where there is a lack of storage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">出售多余机器能腾出空闲空间，并在储存空间不足时节约费用。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A lack of demand left spare capacity, making some equipment redundant despite the energy <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=saving&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>saving</strong></a> it offered.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">需求不足造成产能闲置，一些设备虽能节能，却仍变得多余。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The office reused spare desks to address a lack of furniture, achieving a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=saving&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>saving</strong></a> without buying redundant stock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">办公室利用闲置桌子解决家具不足，实现了节约，也避免买入多余库存。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a cost saving</span> · 成本节省</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=storage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">storage</a></span> · 储存</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=efficiency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">efficiency</a></span> · 效率</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-069">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-069" class="vocabulary-word-title">redundant<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-069" aria-label="定位 redundant">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-069" data-mark-label="redundant" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="redundant：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="redundant：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=redundant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 redundant" title="在有道查询 redundant">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-069</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.多余的；被裁减的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Selling <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=redundant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>redundant</strong></a> machines creates spare space and a saving where there is a lack of storage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">出售多余机器能腾出空闲空间，并在储存空间不足时节约费用。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A lack of demand left spare capacity, making some equipment <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=redundant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>redundant</strong></a> despite the energy saving it offered.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">需求不足造成产能闲置，一些设备虽能节能，却仍变得多余。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The office reused spare desks to address a lack of furniture, achieving a saving without buying <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=redundant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>redundant</strong></a> stock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">办公室利用闲置桌子解决家具不足，实现了节约，也避免买入多余库存。</p></li>

### 词群 16.08｜企业怎样投入和交付产品 {#group-16-08}

**串联方式：** 辨认企业与代理主体，再看投资、投入、产品、收购、货物以及保险和保证。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-071">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-071" class="vocabulary-word-title">company<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-071" aria-label="定位 company">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-071" data-mark-label="company" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="company：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="company：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=company&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 company" title="在有道查询 company">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-071</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.公司；陪伴；剧团</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The agent explained that the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=company&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>company</strong></a>, unlike the informal firm, was a separate legal entity running a social enterprise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">代理人解释，该公司与那家非正式商号不同，是经营社会企业的独立法律实体。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-076">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-076" class="vocabulary-word-title">invest<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-076" aria-label="定位 invest">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-076" data-mark-label="invest" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="invest：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="invest：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 invest" title="在有道查询 invest">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-076</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.投资；投入</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We plan to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>invest</strong></a> in a product whose development requires less capital input than an acquisition.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们计划投资一款产品，其开发所需资本投入少于一次收购。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the acquisition, the owners will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>invest</strong></a> more in product design and seek input from customers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收购后，所有者将加大产品设计投入，并征求客户意见。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The acquisition allows us to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>invest</strong></a> in better tools, reduce material input and improve the final product.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这次收购使我们能投资更好的工具，减少材料投入，并改进最终产品。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">invest in technology</span> · 投资技术</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=development&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">development</a></span> · 开发</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=customer%20feedback&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">customer feedback</a></span> · 客户反馈</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the acquisition, the owners will invest more in product design and seek <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=input&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>input</strong></a> from customers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收购后，所有者将加大产品设计投入，并征求客户意见。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We plan to invest in a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=product&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>product</strong></a> whose development requires less capital input than an acquisition.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们计划投资一款产品，其开发所需资本投入少于一次收购。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the acquisition, the owners will invest more in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=product&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>product</strong></a> design and seek input from customers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收购后，所有者将加大产品设计投入，并征求客户意见。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The acquisition allows us to invest in better tools, reduce material input and improve the final <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=product&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>product</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这次收购使我们能投资更好的工具，减少材料投入，并改进最终产品。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">launch a product</span> · 推出产品</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=development&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">development</a></span> · 开发</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=customer%20feedback&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">customer feedback</a></span> · 客户反馈</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-079">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-079" class="vocabulary-word-title">acquisition<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-079" aria-label="定位 acquisition">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-079" data-mark-label="acquisition" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="acquisition：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="acquisition：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=acquisition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 acquisition" title="在有道查询 acquisition">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-079</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.获得；(对公司的)并购</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We plan to invest in a product whose development requires less capital input than an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=acquisition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>acquisition</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们计划投资一款产品，其开发所需资本投入少于一次收购。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=acquisition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>acquisition</strong></a>, the owners will invest more in product design and seek input from customers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收购后，所有者将加大产品设计投入，并征求客户意见。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=acquisition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>acquisition</strong></a> allows us to invest in better tools, reduce material input and improve the final product.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这次收购使我们能投资更好的工具，减少材料投入，并改进最终产品。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a company acquisition</span> · 公司收购</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=development&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">development</a></span> · 开发</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=customer%20feedback&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">customer feedback</a></span> · 客户反馈</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-080">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-080" class="vocabulary-word-title">goods<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-080" aria-label="定位 goods">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-080" data-mark-label="goods" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="goods：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="goods：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=goods&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 goods" title="在有道查询 goods">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-080</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 商品，货物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A reliable carrier will insure the cargo, but cannot guarantee that fragile <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=goods&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>goods</strong></a> never break.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">可靠承运人会为货物投保，但无法保证易碎商品绝不破损。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Choose a reliable supplier, insure valuable cargo and request a guarantee for damaged <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=goods&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>goods</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">选择可靠供应商，为贵重货物投保，并要求对受损商品提供保证。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We insure every cargo shipment and guarantee that a reliable team checks the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=goods&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>goods</strong></a> before departure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们为每批货物投保，并保证由可靠团队在出发前检查商品。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">consumer goods</span> · 消费品</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shipment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shipment</a></span> · 装运货物</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carrier&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">carrier</a></span> · 承运人</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-081">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-081" class="vocabulary-word-title">cargo<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-081" aria-label="定位 cargo">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-081" data-mark-label="cargo" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="cargo：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="cargo：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cargo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 cargo" title="在有道查询 cargo">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-081</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.货物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A reliable carrier will insure the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cargo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cargo</strong></a>, but cannot guarantee that fragile goods never break.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">可靠承运人会为货物投保，但无法保证易碎商品绝不破损。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Choose a reliable supplier, insure valuable <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cargo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cargo</strong></a> and request a guarantee for damaged goods.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">选择可靠供应商，为贵重货物投保，并要求对受损商品提供保证。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We insure every <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cargo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cargo</strong></a> shipment and guarantee that a reliable team checks the goods before departure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们为每批货物投保，并保证由可靠团队在出发前检查商品。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">air cargo</span> · 空运货物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shipment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shipment</a></span> · 装运货物</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carrier&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">carrier</a></span> · 承运人</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-082">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-082" class="vocabulary-word-title">insure<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-082" aria-label="定位 insure">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-082" data-mark-label="insure" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="insure：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="insure：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=insure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 insure" title="在有道查询 insure">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-082</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.确保；给......上保险</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A reliable carrier will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=insure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>insure</strong></a> the cargo, but cannot guarantee that fragile goods never break.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">可靠承运人会为货物投保，但无法保证易碎商品绝不破损。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Choose a reliable supplier, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=insure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>insure</strong></a> valuable cargo and request a guarantee for damaged goods.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">选择可靠供应商，为贵重货物投保，并要求对受损商品提供保证。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=insure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>insure</strong></a> every cargo shipment and guarantee that a reliable team checks the goods before departure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们为每批货物投保，并保证由可靠团队在出发前检查商品。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">insure the shipment</span> · 为货物投保</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shipment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shipment</a></span> · 装运货物</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carrier&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">carrier</a></span> · 承运人</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-083">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-083" class="vocabulary-word-title">guarantee<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-083" aria-label="定位 guarantee">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-083" data-mark-label="guarantee" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="guarantee：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="guarantee：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=guarantee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 guarantee" title="在有道查询 guarantee">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-083</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.担保；确保；n.保证；保修单；担保金，担保物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A reliable carrier will insure the cargo, but cannot <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=guarantee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>guarantee</strong></a> that fragile goods never break.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">可靠承运人会为货物投保，但无法保证易碎商品绝不破损。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Choose a reliable supplier, insure valuable cargo and request a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=guarantee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>guarantee</strong></a> for damaged goods.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">选择可靠供应商，为贵重货物投保，并要求对受损商品提供保证。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We insure every cargo shipment and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=guarantee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>guarantee</strong></a> that a reliable team checks the goods before departure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们为每批货物投保，并保证由可靠团队在出发前检查商品。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a money-back guarantee</span> · 退款保证</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shipment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shipment</a></span> · 装运货物</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carrier&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">carrier</a></span> · 承运人</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-084">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-084" class="vocabulary-word-title">reliable<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-084" aria-label="定位 reliable">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-084" data-mark-label="reliable" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reliable：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reliable：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reliable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reliable" title="在有道查询 reliable">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-084</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.可靠的，可信赖的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reliable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reliable</strong></a> carrier will insure the cargo, but cannot guarantee that fragile goods never break.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">可靠承运人会为货物投保，但无法保证易碎商品绝不破损。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Choose a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reliable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reliable</strong></a> supplier, insure valuable cargo and request a guarantee for damaged goods.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">选择可靠供应商，为贵重货物投保，并要求对受损商品提供保证。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We insure every cargo shipment and guarantee that a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reliable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reliable</strong></a> team checks the goods before departure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们为每批货物投保，并保证由可靠团队在出发前检查商品。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a reliable supplier</span> · 可靠供应商</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shipment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shipment</a></span> · 装运货物</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carrier&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">carrier</a></span> · 承运人</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 16.09｜谈判怎样形成交易 {#group-16-09}

**串联方式：** 从出席谈判到销售、拍卖、出价、成交，再看营业额、倾销、配额、份额和收益。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-085">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-085" class="vocabulary-word-title">negotiation<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-085" aria-label="定位 negotiation">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-085" data-mark-label="negotiation" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="negotiation：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="negotiation：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=negotiation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 negotiation" title="在有道查询 negotiation">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-085</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.谈判，协商</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The director cannot attend the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=negotiation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>negotiation</strong></a> because she is absent on sick leave.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">主管因病休假，不能出席谈判。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Anyone absent from the preliminary <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=negotiation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>negotiation</strong></a> should attend the final meeting.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">缺席初步谈判的人应参加最后的会议。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Although one adviser was absent, both parties agreed to attend another <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=negotiation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>negotiation</strong></a> next week.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">虽然一位顾问缺席，双方仍同意下周参加另一轮谈判。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a negotiation process</span> · 谈判过程</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=representative&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">representative</a></span> · 代表</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=agreement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">agreement</a></span> · 协议</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-086">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The director cannot <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attend</strong></a> the negotiation because she is absent on sick leave.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">主管因病休假，不能出席谈判。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Anyone absent from the preliminary negotiation should <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attend</strong></a> the final meeting.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">缺席初步谈判的人应参加最后的会议。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Although one adviser was absent, both parties agreed to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attend</strong></a> another negotiation next week.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">虽然一位顾问缺席，双方仍同意下周参加另一轮谈判。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">attend a meeting</span> · 出席会议</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-087">

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.不在场的，缺席的；心不在焉的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The director cannot attend the negotiation because she is <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=absent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>absent</strong></a> on sick leave.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">主管因病休假，不能出席谈判。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Anyone <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=absent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>absent</strong></a> from the preliminary negotiation should attend the final meeting.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">缺席初步谈判的人应参加最后的会议。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Although one adviser was <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=absent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>absent</strong></a>, both parties agreed to attend another negotiation next week.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">虽然一位顾问缺席，双方仍同意下周参加另一轮谈判。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">absent from work</span> · 缺勤</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-088">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-088" class="vocabulary-word-title">sale<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-088" aria-label="定位 sale">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-088" data-mark-label="sale" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sale：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sale：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sale" title="在有道查询 sale">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-088</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.卖，销售；销售额</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the auction, buyers can bid for goods offered for <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sale</strong></a> at wholesale prices.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在拍卖会上，买家可竞价购买以批发价出售的商品。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The wholesale company put its warehouse up for <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sale</strong></a> and accepted the highest bid at auction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">批发公司拍卖仓库，并接受了最高出价。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Check wholesale prices before you bid at an auction <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sale</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在拍卖会上出价前，请查一查批发价。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">for sale</span> · 出售中</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=buyer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">buyer</a></span> · 买方</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">lot</a></span> · 拍卖品</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-089">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-089" class="vocabulary-word-title">wholesale<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-089" aria-label="定位 wholesale">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-089" data-mark-label="wholesale" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="wholesale：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="wholesale：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wholesale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 wholesale" title="在有道查询 wholesale">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-089</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.批发</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the auction, buyers can bid for goods offered for sale at <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wholesale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wholesale</strong></a> prices.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在拍卖会上，买家可竞价购买以批发价出售的商品。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wholesale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wholesale</strong></a> company put its warehouse up for sale and accepted the highest bid at auction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">批发公司拍卖仓库，并接受了最高出价。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Check <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wholesale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wholesale</strong></a> prices before you bid at an auction sale.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在拍卖会上出价前，请查一查批发价。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">at wholesale prices</span> · 以批发价</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=buyer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">buyer</a></span> · 买方</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">lot</a></span> · 拍卖品</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-090">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-090" class="vocabulary-word-title">auction<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-090" aria-label="定位 auction">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-090" data-mark-label="auction" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="auction：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="auction：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=auction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 auction" title="在有道查询 auction">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-090</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.拍卖；n.拍卖</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=auction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>auction</strong></a>, buyers can bid for goods offered for sale at wholesale prices.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在拍卖会上，买家可竞价购买以批发价出售的商品。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The wholesale company put its warehouse up for sale and accepted the highest bid at <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=auction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>auction</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">批发公司拍卖仓库，并接受了最高出价。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Check wholesale prices before you bid at an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=auction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>auction</strong></a> sale.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在拍卖会上出价前，请查一查批发价。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an auction house</span> · 拍卖行</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=buyer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">buyer</a></span> · 买方</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">lot</a></span> · 拍卖品</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-091">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-091" class="vocabulary-word-title">bid<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-091" aria-label="定位 bid">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-091" data-mark-label="bid" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bid：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bid：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bid" title="在有道查询 bid">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-091</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.出价；投标；n.出价；投标</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the auction, buyers can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bid</strong></a> for goods offered for sale at wholesale prices.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在拍卖会上，买家可竞价购买以批发价出售的商品。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The wholesale company put its warehouse up for sale and accepted the highest <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bid</strong></a> at auction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">批发公司拍卖仓库，并接受了最高出价。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Check wholesale prices before you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bid</strong></a> at an auction sale.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在拍卖会上出价前，请查一查批发价。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">place a bid</span> · 出价</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=buyer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">buyer</a></span> · 买方</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">lot</a></span> · 拍卖品</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-092">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-092" class="vocabulary-word-title">deal<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-092" aria-label="定位 deal">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-092" data-mark-label="deal" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="deal：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="deal：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 deal" title="在有道查询 deal">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-092</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.买卖；［~with］处理，应付；n.协议，交易；大量</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deal</strong></a> involves a single transaction worth half the firm's annual turnover.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这份协议涉及一笔相当于公司年营业额一半的交易。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A successful transaction can increase turnover, but a poorly priced <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deal</strong></a> may reduce profit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">成功的交易能增加营业额，但定价不当的协议可能降低利润。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before approving the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deal</strong></a>, auditors compared the transaction value with reported turnover.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">批准协议前，审计人员将交易价值与申报营业额作了比较。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">close a deal</span> · 达成交易</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=auditor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">auditor</a></span> · 审计员</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=profit%20margin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">profit margin</a></span> · 利润率</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-093">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-093" class="vocabulary-word-title">transaction<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-093" aria-label="定位 transaction">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-093" data-mark-label="transaction" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="transaction：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="transaction：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transaction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 transaction" title="在有道查询 transaction">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-093</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. (一笔)交易，业务</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The deal involves a single <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transaction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>transaction</strong></a> worth half the firm's annual turnover.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这份协议涉及一笔相当于公司年营业额一半的交易。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A successful <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transaction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>transaction</strong></a> can increase turnover, but a poorly priced deal may reduce profit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">成功的交易能增加营业额，但定价不当的协议可能降低利润。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before approving the deal, auditors compared the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transaction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>transaction</strong></a> value with reported turnover.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">批准协议前，审计人员将交易价值与申报营业额作了比较。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a business transaction</span> · 商业交易</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=auditor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">auditor</a></span> · 审计员</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=profit%20margin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">profit margin</a></span> · 利润率</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-094">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-094" class="vocabulary-word-title">turnover<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-094" aria-label="定位 turnover">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-094" data-mark-label="turnover" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="turnover：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="turnover：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=turnover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 turnover" title="在有道查询 turnover">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-094</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. (人员)流动率；营业额</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The deal involves a single transaction worth half the firm's annual <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=turnover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>turnover</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这份协议涉及一笔相当于公司年营业额一半的交易。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A successful transaction can increase <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=turnover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>turnover</strong></a>, but a poorly priced deal may reduce profit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">成功的交易能增加营业额，但定价不当的协议可能降低利润。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before approving the deal, auditors compared the transaction value with reported <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=turnover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>turnover</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">批准协议前，审计人员将交易价值与申报营业额作了比较。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">annual turnover</span> · 年营业额</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=auditor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">auditor</a></span> · 审计员</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=profit%20margin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">profit margin</a></span> · 利润率</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-095">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-095" class="vocabulary-word-title">dump<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-095" aria-label="定位 dump">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-095" data-mark-label="dump" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="dump：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="dump：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dump&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 dump" title="在有道查询 dump">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-095</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.丢弃，倾倒；倾销</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Exporters may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dump&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dump</strong></a> surplus goods abroad when a domestic quota limits sales.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">国内配额限制销售时，出口商可能向国外倾销过剩商品。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The import quota made it harder to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dump&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dump</strong></a> cheap steel in the local market.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">进口配额让向本地市场倾销廉价钢材更困难。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials accused the firm of trying to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dump&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dump</strong></a> stock before a new quota took effect.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员指责该公司试图在新配额生效前倾销库存。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">dump surplus goods</span> · 倾销过剩商品</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surplus&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">surplus</a></span> · 过剩</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trade%20restriction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">trade restriction</a></span> · 贸易限制</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-096">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-096" class="vocabulary-word-title">quota<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-096" aria-label="定位 quota">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-096" data-mark-label="quota" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="quota：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="quota：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quota&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 quota" title="在有道查询 quota">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-096</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.限额，配额；定额</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Exporters may dump surplus goods abroad when a domestic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quota&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>quota</strong></a> limits sales.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">国内配额限制销售时，出口商可能向国外倾销过剩商品。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The import <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quota&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>quota</strong></a> made it harder to dump cheap steel in the local market.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">进口配额让向本地市场倾销廉价钢材更困难。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials accused the firm of trying to dump stock before a new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quota&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>quota</strong></a> took effect.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员指责该公司试图在新配额生效前倾销库存。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an import quota</span> · 进口配额</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surplus&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">surplus</a></span> · 过剩</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trade%20restriction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">trade restriction</a></span> · 贸易限制</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-097">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-097" class="vocabulary-word-title">share<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-097" aria-label="定位 share">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-097" data-mark-label="share" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="share：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="share：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=share&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 share" title="在有道查询 share">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-097</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.股份，股票；(在若干人之间分得的)一份；v.分享；共享；分担</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=share&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>share</strong></a> in the stock fund offers a possible benefit, while a deposit earns interest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">持有股票基金份额可能带来收益，而存款可赚取利息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Employees who own a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=share&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>share</strong></a> of company stock benefit when profits rise, but receive no guaranteed interest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">持有公司股票份额的员工能从利润增长中受益，但没有保证利息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The adviser compared the benefit of buying a stock <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=share&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>share</strong></a> with the interest available on savings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">顾问比较了买入股票份额的收益与储蓄可得的利息。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a market share</span> · 市场份额</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investment%20return&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investment return</a></span> · 投资回报</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=savings%20account&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">savings account</a></span> · 储蓄账户</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-098">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-098" class="vocabulary-word-title">benefit<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-098" aria-label="定位 benefit">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-098" data-mark-label="benefit" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="benefit：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="benefit：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=benefit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 benefit" title="在有道查询 benefit">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-098</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.益处；v.有利于；获益</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A share in the stock fund offers a possible <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=benefit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>benefit</strong></a>, while a deposit earns interest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">持有股票基金份额可能带来收益，而存款可赚取利息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Employees who own a share of company stock <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=benefit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>benefit</strong></a> when profits rise, but receive no guaranteed interest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">持有公司股票份额的员工能从利润增长中受益，但没有保证利息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The adviser compared the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=benefit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>benefit</strong></a> of buying a stock share with the interest available on savings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">顾问比较了买入股票份额的收益与储蓄可得的利息。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a mutual benefit</span> · 共同利益</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investment%20return&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investment return</a></span> · 投资回报</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=savings%20account&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">savings account</a></span> · 储蓄账户</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-099">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-099" class="vocabulary-word-title">interest<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-099" aria-label="定位 interest">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-099" data-mark-label="interest" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="interest：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="interest：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=interest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 interest" title="在有道查询 interest">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-099</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.兴趣；利益；利息；v.使感兴趣</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A share in the stock fund offers a possible benefit, while a deposit earns <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=interest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>interest</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">持有股票基金份额可能带来收益，而存款可赚取利息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Employees who own a share of company stock benefit when profits rise, but receive no guaranteed <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=interest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>interest</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">持有公司股票份额的员工能从利润增长中受益，但没有保证利息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The adviser compared the benefit of buying a stock share with the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=interest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>interest</strong></a> available on savings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">顾问比较了买入股票份额的收益与储蓄可得的利息。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">earn interest</span> · 赚取利息</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investment%20return&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investment return</a></span> · 投资回报</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=savings%20account&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">savings account</a></span> · 储蓄账户</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-100">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-100" class="vocabulary-word-title">stock<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-100" aria-label="定位 stock">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-100" data-mark-label="stock" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="stock：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="stock：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 stock" title="在有道查询 stock">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-100</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.股本:股票:库存</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A share in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stock</strong></a> fund offers a possible benefit, while a deposit earns interest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">持有股票基金份额可能带来收益，而存款可赚取利息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Employees who own a share of company <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stock</strong></a> benefit when profits rise, but receive no guaranteed interest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">持有公司股票份额的员工能从利润增长中受益，但没有保证利息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The adviser compared the benefit of buying a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stock</strong></a> share with the interest available on savings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">顾问比较了买入股票份额的收益与储蓄可得的利息。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">stock market</span> · 股票市场</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investment%20return&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investment return</a></span> · 投资回报</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=savings%20account&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">savings account</a></span> · 储蓄账户</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 16.10｜钱怎样存放、积累与转移 {#group-16-10}

**串联方式：** 从账户、存款、分期和支付工具进入财富、财产、继承及退款。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-101">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-101" class="vocabulary-word-title">account<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-101" aria-label="定位 account">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-101" data-mark-label="account" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="account：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="account：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=account&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 account" title="在有道查询 account">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-101</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.账户;解释</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A deposit left in your <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=account&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>account</strong></a> can cover the first instalment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">留在账户里的存款可以支付首期款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The bank will deduct each instalment from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=account&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>account</strong></a> after receiving the initial deposit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收到首笔存款后，银行将从账户中扣除每期款项。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Check your <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=account&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>account</strong></a> balance before paying the deposit and agreeing to a monthly instalment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">支付押金并同意每月分期付款前，请查看账户余额。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">open an account</span> · 开户</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bank%20balance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bank balance</a></span> · 银行余额</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repayment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repayment</a></span> · 还款</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-102">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-102" class="vocabulary-word-title">deposit<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-102" aria-label="定位 deposit">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-102" data-mark-label="deposit" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="deposit：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="deposit：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deposit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 deposit" title="在有道查询 deposit">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-102</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.存款;押金</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deposit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deposit</strong></a> left in your account can cover the first instalment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">留在账户里的存款可以支付首期款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The bank will deduct each instalment from the account after receiving the initial <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deposit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deposit</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收到首笔存款后，银行将从账户中扣除每期款项。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Check your account balance before paying the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deposit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deposit</strong></a> and agreeing to a monthly instalment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">支付押金并同意每月分期付款前，请查看账户余额。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">pay a deposit</span> · 交押金</p>

</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-103">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-103" class="vocabulary-word-title">instalment<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-103" aria-label="定位 instalment">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-103" data-mark-label="instalment" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="instalment：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="instalment：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=instalment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 instalment" title="在有道查询 instalment">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-103</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A deposit left in your account can cover the first <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=instalment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>instalment</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">留在账户里的存款可以支付首期款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The bank will deduct each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=instalment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>instalment</strong></a> from the account after receiving the initial deposit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收到首笔存款后，银行将从账户中扣除每期款项。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Check your account balance before paying the deposit and agreeing to a monthly <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=instalment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>instalment</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">支付押金并同意每月分期付款前，请查看账户余额。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a monthly instalment</span> · 每月分期款</p>
