<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-104">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-104" class="vocabulary-word-title">cheque<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-104" aria-label="定位 cheque">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-104" data-mark-label="cheque" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="cheque：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="cheque：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cheque&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 cheque" title="在有道查询 cheque">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-104</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">(=check)；n.支票</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum accepts cash or a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cheque&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cheque</strong></a> for its exhibition of the shilling, the copper coin and other historic money.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆的先令、铜币等历史货币展接受现金或支票付款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She paid cash rather than a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cheque&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cheque</strong></a> for a copper coin displayed beside an old shilling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她购买了与一枚旧先令并排展出的铜币，用现金而非支票付款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A collector offered cash and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cheque&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cheque</strong></a> in exchange for the copper coin and the rare shilling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收藏家提出用现金加支票换那枚铜币和稀有先令。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">write a cheque</span> · 开支票</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collector&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">collector</a></span> · 收藏家</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=currency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">currency</a></span> · 货币</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-105">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-105" class="vocabulary-word-title">cash<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-105" aria-label="定位 cash">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-105" data-mark-label="cash" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="cash：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="cash：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 cash" title="在有道查询 cash">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-105</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.现金；v.兑现(支票)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum accepts <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cash</strong></a> or a cheque for its exhibition of the shilling, the copper coin and other historic money.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆的先令、铜币等历史货币展接受现金或支票付款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She paid <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cash</strong></a> rather than a cheque for a copper coin displayed beside an old shilling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她购买了与一枚旧先令并排展出的铜币，用现金而非支票付款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A collector offered <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cash</strong></a> and a cheque in exchange for the copper coin and the rare shilling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收藏家提出用现金加支票换那枚铜币和稀有先令。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">pay in cash</span> · 用现金支付</p>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.硬币 v铸币;创造(新词语)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum accepts cash or a cheque for its exhibition of the shilling, the copper <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>coin</strong></a> and other historic money.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆的先令、铜币等历史货币展接受现金或支票付款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She paid cash rather than a cheque for a copper <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>coin</strong></a> displayed beside an old shilling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她购买了与一枚旧先令并排展出的铜币，用现金而非支票付款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A collector offered cash and a cheque in exchange for the copper <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>coin</strong></a> and the rare shilling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收藏家提出用现金加支票换那枚铜币和稀有先令。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-107">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-107" class="vocabulary-word-title">shilling<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-107" aria-label="定位 shilling">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-107" data-mark-label="shilling" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="shilling：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="shilling：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shilling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 shilling" title="在有道查询 shilling">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-107</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.先令</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum accepts cash or a cheque for its exhibition of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shilling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shilling</strong></a>, the copper coin and other historic money.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆的先令、铜币等历史货币展接受现金或支票付款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She paid cash rather than a cheque for a copper coin displayed beside an old <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shilling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shilling</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她购买了与一枚旧先令并排展出的铜币，用现金而非支票付款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A collector offered cash and a cheque in exchange for the copper coin and the rare <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shilling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shilling</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收藏家提出用现金加支票换那枚铜币和稀有先令。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an old shilling</span> · 旧先令</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collector&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">collector</a></span> · 收藏家</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=currency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">currency</a></span> · 货币</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum accepts cash or a cheque for its exhibition of the shilling, the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=copper&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>copper</strong></a> coin and other historic money.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆的先令、铜币等历史货币展接受现金或支票付款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She paid cash rather than a cheque for a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=copper&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>copper</strong></a> coin displayed beside an old shilling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她购买了与一枚旧先令并排展出的铜币，用现金而非支票付款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A collector offered cash and a cheque in exchange for the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=copper&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>copper</strong></a> coin and the rare shilling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收藏家提出用现金加支票换那枚铜币和稀有先令。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-109">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-109" class="vocabulary-word-title">treasure<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-109" aria-label="定位 treasure">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-109" data-mark-label="treasure" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="treasure：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="treasure：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=treasure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 treasure" title="在有道查询 treasure">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-109</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.金银财宝;宝物，珍品</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The estate contained a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=treasure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>treasure</strong></a> collection, but her lasting legacy was the wealth of knowledge she helped others possess.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">遗产中有珍宝收藏，但她长久的遗产是她帮助他人拥有的丰富知识。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To possess inherited wealth, he had to manage an estate whose principal legacy was a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=treasure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>treasure</strong></a> of rare books.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为拥有继承的财富，他必须管理一处地产，其中主要遗产是珍贵藏书。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The family used its wealth to preserve the estate and its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=treasure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>treasure</strong></a>, creating a legacy that future generations could possess.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这家人用财富保护庄园及珍宝，创造了后代能拥有的遗产。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">buried treasure</span> · 埋藏的财宝</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inheritance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inheritance</a></span> · 继承</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preservation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">preservation</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-110">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-110" class="vocabulary-word-title">wealth<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-110" aria-label="定位 wealth">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-110" data-mark-label="wealth" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="wealth：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="wealth：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wealth&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 wealth" title="在有道查询 wealth">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-110</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.财富，丰富</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The estate contained a treasure collection, but her lasting legacy was the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wealth&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wealth</strong></a> of knowledge she helped others possess.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">遗产中有珍宝收藏，但她长久的遗产是她帮助他人拥有的丰富知识。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To possess inherited <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wealth&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wealth</strong></a>, he had to manage an estate whose principal legacy was a treasure of rare books.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为拥有继承的财富，他必须管理一处地产，其中主要遗产是珍贵藏书。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The family used its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wealth&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wealth</strong></a> to preserve the estate and its treasure, creating a legacy that future generations could possess.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这家人用财富保护庄园及珍宝，创造了后代能拥有的遗产。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">accumulate wealth</span> · 积累财富</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inheritance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inheritance</a></span> · 继承</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preservation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">preservation</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-111">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.地产；遗产</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=estate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>estate</strong></a> contained a treasure collection, but her lasting legacy was the wealth of knowledge she helped others possess.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">遗产中有珍宝收藏，但她长久的遗产是她帮助他人拥有的丰富知识。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To possess inherited wealth, he had to manage an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=estate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>estate</strong></a> whose principal legacy was a treasure of rare books.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为拥有继承的财富，他必须管理一处地产，其中主要遗产是珍贵藏书。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The family used its wealth to preserve the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=estate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>estate</strong></a> and its treasure, creating a legacy that future generations could possess.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这家人用财富保护庄园及珍宝，创造了后代能拥有的遗产。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">inherit an estate</span> · 继承遗产</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inheritance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inheritance</a></span> · 继承</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preservation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">preservation</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>

</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-112">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The estate contained a treasure collection, but her lasting legacy was the wealth of knowledge she helped others <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=possess&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>possess</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">遗产中有珍宝收藏，但她长久的遗产是她帮助他人拥有的丰富知识。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=possess&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>possess</strong></a> inherited wealth, he had to manage an estate whose principal legacy was a treasure of rare books.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为拥有继承的财富，他必须管理一处地产，其中主要遗产是珍贵藏书。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The family used its wealth to preserve the estate and its treasure, creating a legacy that future generations could <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=possess&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>possess</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这家人用财富保护庄园及珍宝，创造了后代能拥有的遗产。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">possess property</span> · 拥有财产</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inheritance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inheritance</a></span> · 继承</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preservation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">preservation</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The estate contained a treasure collection, but her lasting <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=legacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>legacy</strong></a> was the wealth of knowledge she helped others possess.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">遗产中有珍宝收藏，但她长久的遗产是她帮助他人拥有的丰富知识。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To possess inherited wealth, he had to manage an estate whose principal <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=legacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>legacy</strong></a> was a treasure of rare books.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为拥有继承的财富，他必须管理一处地产，其中主要遗产是珍贵藏书。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The family used its wealth to preserve the estate and its treasure, creating a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=legacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>legacy</strong></a> that future generations could possess.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这家人用财富保护庄园及珍宝，创造了后代能拥有的遗产。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-114">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-114" class="vocabulary-word-title">descend<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-114" aria-label="定位 descend">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-114" data-mark-label="descend" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="descend：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="descend：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=descend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 descend" title="在有道查询 descend">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-114</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 下降，下来</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Please <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=descend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>descend</strong></a> to the ground floor to request a refund.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">请下到一楼申请退款。</p></li>

### 词群 16.12｜工作怎样获得报酬 {#group-16-12}

**串联方式：** 区分薪水、收入、工资、支付和报销，再看工资表、报酬、补贴与赚取。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-124">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-124" class="vocabulary-word-title">salary<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-124" aria-label="定位 salary">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-124" data-mark-label="salary" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="salary：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="salary：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=salary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 salary" title="在有道查询 salary">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-124</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.工资，(尤指按月领的)薪水</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=salary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>salary</strong></a> forms most of the household income, while her son's wage covers his travel.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的薪水构成家庭收入的大部分，儿子的工资则支付他的交通费。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey records income from a monthly <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=salary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>salary</strong></a>, an hourly wage and other sources.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查记录月薪、时薪及其他来源的收入。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A higher minimum wage can raise income for workers who do not receive a fixed <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=salary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>salary</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">提高最低工资能增加没有固定月薪的工人的收入。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an annual salary</span> · 年薪</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=earnings&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">earnings</a></span> · 收入</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=employment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">employment</a></span> · 就业</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-125">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-125" class="vocabulary-word-title">income<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-125" aria-label="定位 income">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-125" data-mark-label="income" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="income：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="income：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=income&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 income" title="在有道查询 income">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-125</span></span></summary>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey records <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=income&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>income</strong></a> from a monthly salary, an hourly wage and other sources.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查记录月薪、时薪及其他来源的收入。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A higher minimum wage can raise <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=income&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>income</strong></a> for workers who do not receive a fixed salary.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">提高最低工资能增加没有固定月薪的工人的收入。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey records income from a monthly salary, an hourly <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wage</strong></a> and other sources.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查记录月薪、时薪及其他来源的收入。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A higher minimum <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wage</strong></a> can raise income for workers who do not receive a fixed salary.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">提高最低工资能增加没有固定月薪的工人的收入。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-127">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-127" class="vocabulary-word-title">payment<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-127" aria-label="定位 payment">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-127" data-mark-label="payment" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="payment：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="payment：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=payment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 payment" title="在有道查询 payment">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-127</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.支付；支付的款顶；付款额</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The payroll team will reimburse travel expenses through a separate <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=payment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>payment</strong></a> from regular remuneration.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">薪资团队将通过独立于正常报酬的付款报销差旅费。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fair remuneration requires accurate payroll records and prompt <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=payment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>payment</strong></a>, including a system to reimburse expenses.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公平报酬需要准确的工资记录、及时付款以及费用报销制度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before the next <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=payment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>payment</strong></a>, payroll staff must calculate remuneration and reimburse approved costs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">下次付款前，薪资人员必须计算报酬并报销已批准的费用。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">make a payment</span> · 付款</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expense%20claim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">expense claim</a></span> · 报销申请</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pay%20slip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">pay slip</a></span> · 工资单</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-128">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-128" class="vocabulary-word-title">reimburse<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-128" aria-label="定位 reimburse">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-128" data-mark-label="reimburse" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reimburse：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reimburse：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reimburse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reimburse" title="在有道查询 reimburse">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-128</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 赔偿，补偿</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The payroll team will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reimburse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reimburse</strong></a> travel expenses through a separate payment from regular remuneration.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">薪资团队将通过独立于正常报酬的付款报销差旅费。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fair remuneration requires accurate payroll records and prompt payment, including a system to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reimburse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reimburse</strong></a> expenses.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公平报酬需要准确的工资记录、及时付款以及费用报销制度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before the next payment, payroll staff must calculate remuneration and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reimburse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reimburse</strong></a> approved costs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">下次付款前，薪资人员必须计算报酬并报销已批准的费用。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reimburse expenses</span> · 报销费用</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-129">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-129" class="vocabulary-word-title">payroll<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-129" aria-label="定位 payroll">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-129" data-mark-label="payroll" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="payroll：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="payroll：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=payroll&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 payroll" title="在有道查询 payroll">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-129</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.工资名单</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=payroll&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>payroll</strong></a> team will reimburse travel expenses through a separate payment from regular remuneration.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">薪资团队将通过独立于正常报酬的付款报销差旅费。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fair remuneration requires accurate <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=payroll&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>payroll</strong></a> records and prompt payment, including a system to reimburse expenses.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公平报酬需要准确的工资记录、及时付款以及费用报销制度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before the next payment, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=payroll&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>payroll</strong></a> staff must calculate remuneration and reimburse approved costs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">下次付款前，薪资人员必须计算报酬并报销已批准的费用。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">on the payroll</span> · 在工资名单上</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expense%20claim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">expense claim</a></span> · 报销申请</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pay%20slip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">pay slip</a></span> · 工资单</li></ul>

</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-130">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-130" class="vocabulary-word-title">remuneration<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-130" aria-label="定位 remuneration">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-130" data-mark-label="remuneration" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="remuneration：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="remuneration：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=remuneration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 remuneration" title="在有道查询 remuneration">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-130</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The payroll team will reimburse travel expenses through a separate payment from regular <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=remuneration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>remuneration</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">薪资团队将通过独立于正常报酬的付款报销差旅费。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fair <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=remuneration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>remuneration</strong></a> requires accurate payroll records and prompt payment, including a system to reimburse expenses.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公平报酬需要准确的工资记录、及时付款以及费用报销制度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before the next payment, payroll staff must calculate <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=remuneration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>remuneration</strong></a> and reimburse approved costs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">下次付款前，薪资人员必须计算报酬并报销已批准的费用。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">fair remuneration</span> · 公平报酬</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-131">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-131" class="vocabulary-word-title">subsidy<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-131" aria-label="定位 subsidy">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-131" data-mark-label="subsidy" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="subsidy：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="subsidy：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=subsidy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 subsidy" title="在有道查询 subsidy">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-131</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.津贴，补贴</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A transport <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=subsidy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>subsidy</strong></a> helps apprentices earn a living while completing their training.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">交通补贴帮助学徒在完成培训的同时谋生。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Farmers may earn less without a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=subsidy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>subsidy</strong></a>, especially after a poor harvest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">没有补贴，农民可能收入更少，特别是在歉收之后。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=subsidy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>subsidy</strong></a> lets young researchers earn enough to remain in the profession.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">补贴让年轻研究者获得足够收入，继续从事这一职业。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a government subsidy</span> · 政府补贴</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=apprenticeship&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">apprenticeship</a></span> · 学徒培训</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=livelihood&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">livelihood</a></span> · 生计</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-132">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A transport subsidy helps apprentices <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=earn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>earn</strong></a> a living while completing their training.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">交通补贴帮助学徒在完成培训的同时谋生。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Farmers may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=earn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>earn</strong></a> less without a subsidy, especially after a poor harvest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">没有补贴，农民可能收入更少，特别是在歉收之后。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The subsidy lets young researchers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=earn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>earn</strong></a> enough to remain in the profession.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">补贴让年轻研究者获得足够收入，继续从事这一职业。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 16.13｜职业与劳动力组织 {#group-16-13}

**串联方式：** 从事业、职业、岗位和志业进入劳动、员工队伍、人事与文员角色。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-133">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-133" class="vocabulary-word-title">undertaking<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-133" aria-label="定位 undertaking">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-133" data-mark-label="undertaking" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="undertaking：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="undertaking：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undertaking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 undertaking" title="在有道查询 undertaking">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-133</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 任务，事业；许诺，保证</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Entering the medical profession is a major <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undertaking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>undertaking</strong></a> that shapes a person's career and occupation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">进入医疗行业是一项重大事业，会影响一个人的职业生涯和职业。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey asks about occupation, career plans and the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undertaking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>undertaking</strong></a> required to enter a chosen profession.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查询问职业、职业规划及进入选定行业所需承担的任务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Changing occupation midway through a career is a serious <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undertaking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>undertaking</strong></a>, especially in a regulated profession.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">职业生涯中途转行是一项重大任务，尤其是在受监管行业。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a major undertaking</span> · 重大任务</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=qualification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">qualification</a></span> · 资格</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=professional%20training&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">professional training</a></span> · 职业培训</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-134">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Entering the medical profession is a major undertaking that shapes a person's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=career&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>career</strong></a> and occupation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">进入医疗行业是一项重大事业，会影响一个人的职业生涯和职业。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey asks about occupation, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=career&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>career</strong></a> plans and the undertaking required to enter a chosen profession.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查询问职业、职业规划及进入选定行业所需承担的任务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Changing occupation midway through a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=career&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>career</strong></a> is a serious undertaking, especially in a regulated profession.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">职业生涯中途转行是一项重大任务，尤其是在受监管行业。</p></li>
</ol>

</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the medical profession</span> · 医疗职业</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=qualification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">qualification</a></span> · 资格</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=professional%20training&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">professional training</a></span> · 职业培训</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-136">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-136" class="vocabulary-word-title">occupation<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-136" aria-label="定位 occupation">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-136" data-mark-label="occupation" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="occupation：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="occupation：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=occupation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 occupation" title="在有道查询 occupation">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-136</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.占领，占据；职业</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Entering the medical profession is a major undertaking that shapes a person's career and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=occupation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>occupation</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">进入医疗行业是一项重大事业，会影响一个人的职业生涯和职业。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey asks about <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=occupation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>occupation</strong></a>, career plans and the undertaking required to enter a chosen profession.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查询问职业、职业规划及进入选定行业所需承担的任务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Changing <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=occupation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>occupation</strong></a> midway through a career is a serious undertaking, especially in a regulated profession.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">职业生涯中途转行是一项重大任务，尤其是在受监管行业。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">state your occupation</span> · 填写职业</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=qualification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">qualification</a></span> · 资格</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=professional%20training&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">professional training</a></span> · 职业培训</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-137">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-137" class="vocabulary-word-title">job<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-137" aria-label="定位 job">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-137" data-mark-label="job" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="job：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="job：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=job&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 job" title="在有道查询 job">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-137</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.职业，工作；(一份)工作，活儿</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A healthy workforce needs fair labour conditions, whether someone sees the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=job&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>job</strong></a> as a vocation or becomes a workaholic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无论有人把工作视为志业还是变成工作狂，健康的员工队伍都需要公平的劳动条件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The workaholic saw his <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=job&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>job</strong></a> as a vocation, but the labour union warned that an exhausted workforce was unsafe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位工作狂将工作视为志业，但工会警告说，疲惫的员工队伍并不安全。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Every <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=job&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>job</strong></a> in the workforce deserves respect; a vocation should never require labour that turns someone into a workaholic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">员工队伍中的每份工作都值得尊重；志业不应要求把人变成工作狂的劳动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">find a job</span> · 找工作</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=work-life%20balance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">work-life balance</a></span> · 工作与生活的平衡</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=union&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">union</a></span> · 工会</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-138">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-138" class="vocabulary-word-title">vocation<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-138" aria-label="定位 vocation">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-138" data-mark-label="vocation" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="vocation：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="vocation：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vocation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 vocation" title="在有道查询 vocation">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-138</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. (认为特别适合自己的)职业；使命感</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A healthy workforce needs fair labour conditions, whether someone sees the job as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vocation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vocation</strong></a> or becomes a workaholic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无论有人把工作视为志业还是变成工作狂，健康的员工队伍都需要公平的劳动条件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The workaholic saw his job as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vocation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vocation</strong></a>, but the labour union warned that an exhausted workforce was unsafe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位工作狂将工作视为志业，但工会警告说，疲惫的员工队伍并不安全。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Every job in the workforce deserves respect; a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vocation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vocation</strong></a> should never require labour that turns someone into a workaholic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">员工队伍中的每份工作都值得尊重；志业不应要求把人变成工作狂的劳动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a sense of vocation</span> · 职业使命感</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=work-life%20balance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">work-life balance</a></span> · 工作与生活的平衡</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=union&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">union</a></span> · 工会</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-139">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-139" class="vocabulary-word-title">labour<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-139" aria-label="定位 labour">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-139" data-mark-label="labour" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="labour：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="labour：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=labour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 labour" title="在有道查询 labour">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-139</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">(= labor)；n.劳动，体力劳动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A healthy workforce needs fair <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=labour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>labour</strong></a> conditions, whether someone sees the job as a vocation or becomes a workaholic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无论有人把工作视为志业还是变成工作狂，健康的员工队伍都需要公平的劳动条件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The workaholic saw his job as a vocation, but the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=labour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>labour</strong></a> union warned that an exhausted workforce was unsafe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位工作狂将工作视为志业，但工会警告说，疲惫的员工队伍并不安全。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Every job in the workforce deserves respect; a vocation should never require <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=labour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>labour</strong></a> that turns someone into a workaholic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">员工队伍中的每份工作都值得尊重；志业不应要求把人变成工作狂的劳动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">manual labour</span> · 体力劳动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=work-life%20balance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">work-life balance</a></span> · 工作与生活的平衡</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=union&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">union</a></span> · 工会</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.劳动力；全体员工</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A healthy <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=workforce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>workforce</strong></a> needs fair labour conditions, whether someone sees the job as a vocation or becomes a workaholic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无论有人把工作视为志业还是变成工作狂，健康的员工队伍都需要公平的劳动条件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The workaholic saw his job as a vocation, but the labour union warned that an exhausted <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=workforce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>workforce</strong></a> was unsafe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位工作狂将工作视为志业，但工会警告说，疲惫的员工队伍并不安全。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Every job in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=workforce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>workforce</strong></a> deserves respect; a vocation should never require labour that turns someone into a workaholic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">员工队伍中的每份工作都值得尊重；志业不应要求把人变成工作狂的劳动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a skilled workforce</span> · 熟练员工队伍</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=work-life%20balance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">work-life balance</a></span> · 工作与生活的平衡</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=union&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">union</a></span> · 工会</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-141">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-141" class="vocabulary-word-title">workaholic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-141" aria-label="定位 workaholic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-141" data-mark-label="workaholic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="workaholic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="workaholic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=workaholic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 workaholic" title="在有道查询 workaholic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-141</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.工作狂</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A healthy workforce needs fair labour conditions, whether someone sees the job as a vocation or becomes a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=workaholic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>workaholic</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无论有人把工作视为志业还是变成工作狂，健康的员工队伍都需要公平的劳动条件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=workaholic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>workaholic</strong></a> saw his job as a vocation, but the labour union warned that an exhausted workforce was unsafe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位工作狂将工作视为志业，但工会警告说，疲惫的员工队伍并不安全。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Every job in the workforce deserves respect; a vocation should never require labour that turns someone into a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=workaholic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>workaholic</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">员工队伍中的每份工作都值得尊重；志业不应要求把人变成工作狂的劳动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a compulsive workaholic</span> · 停不下来的工作狂</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=work-life%20balance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">work-life balance</a></span> · 工作与生活的平衡</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=union&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">union</a></span> · 工会</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-142">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-142" class="vocabulary-word-title">personnel<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-142" aria-label="定位 personnel">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-142" data-mark-label="personnel" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="personnel：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="personnel：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=personnel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 personnel" title="在有道查询 personnel">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-142</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.人员，职员；人事部门</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The clerk updates <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=personnel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>personnel</strong></a> records whenever someone joins the company.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每当有人入职，文员便更新人事档案。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=personnel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Personnel</strong></a> officers asked the clerk to arrange the interviews.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">人事专员请文员安排面试。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A payroll clerk must protect confidential <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=personnel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>personnel</strong></a> information.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">薪资文员必须保护机密人事信息。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">personnel records</span> · 人事档案</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=confidentiality&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">confidentiality</a></span> · 保密</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recruitment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">recruitment</a></span> · 招聘</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-143">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-143" class="vocabulary-word-title">clerk<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-143" aria-label="定位 clerk">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-143" data-mark-label="clerk" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="clerk：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="clerk：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clerk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 clerk" title="在有道查询 clerk">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-143</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.店员；办事员；接待员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clerk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>clerk</strong></a> updates personnel records whenever someone joins the company.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每当有人入职，文员便更新人事档案。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Personnel officers asked the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clerk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>clerk</strong></a> to arrange the interviews.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">人事专员请文员安排面试。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A payroll <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clerk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>clerk</strong></a> must protect confidential personnel information.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">薪资文员必须保护机密人事信息。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an office clerk</span> · 办公室文员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=confidentiality&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">confidentiality</a></span> · 保密</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recruitment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">recruitment</a></span> · 招聘</li></ul>
</div>

### 词群 16.14｜从求职到离职 {#group-16-14}

**串联方式：** 准备面试和简历，经过推荐、雇用，再辨认解雇、裁员、辞职与退休。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-144">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-144" class="vocabulary-word-title">interview<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-144" aria-label="定位 interview">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-144" data-mark-label="interview" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="interview：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="interview：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=interview&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 interview" title="在有道查询 interview">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-144</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.面试；采访；v.对......进行面试；采访</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=interview&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>interview</strong></a>, we will resume discussions and recommend a candidate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">面试后，我们将恢复讨论并推荐候选人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">I recommend that we resume the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=interview&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>interview</strong></a> once the technical problem is resolved.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我建议技术问题解决后继续面试。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The panel will resume work tomorrow and recommend whom to invite for a second <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=interview&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>interview</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">评审组明天将恢复工作，并建议邀请谁参加第二轮面试。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a job interview</span> · 求职面试</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recruiter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">recruiter</a></span> · 招聘者</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=candidate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">candidate</a></span> · 候选人</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-145">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-145" class="vocabulary-word-title">resume<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-145" aria-label="定位 resume">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-145" data-mark-label="resume" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="resume：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="resume：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resume&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 resume" title="在有道查询 resume">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-145</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.重新开始，(中断后)继续</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the interview, we will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resume&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>resume</strong></a> discussions and recommend a candidate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">面试后，我们将恢复讨论并推荐候选人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">I recommend that we <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resume&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>resume</strong></a> the interview once the technical problem is resolved.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我建议技术问题解决后继续面试。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The panel will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resume&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>resume</strong></a> work tomorrow and recommend whom to invite for a second interview.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">评审组明天将恢复工作，并建议邀请谁参加第二轮面试。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">resume work</span> · 恢复工作</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recruiter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">recruiter</a></span> · 招聘者</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=candidate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">candidate</a></span> · 候选人</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-146">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-146" class="vocabulary-word-title">recommend<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-146" aria-label="定位 recommend">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-146" data-mark-label="recommend" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="recommend：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="recommend：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recommend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 recommend" title="在有道查询 recommend">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-146</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the interview, we will resume discussions and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recommend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recommend</strong></a> a candidate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">面试后，我们将恢复讨论并推荐候选人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">I <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recommend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recommend</strong></a> that we resume the interview once the technical problem is resolved.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我建议技术问题解决后继续面试。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The panel will resume work tomorrow and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recommend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recommend</strong></a> whom to invite for a second interview.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">评审组明天将恢复工作，并建议邀请谁参加第二轮面试。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">recommend a candidate</span> · 推荐候选人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recruiter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">recruiter</a></span> · 招聘者</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=candidate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">candidate</a></span> · 候选人</li></ul>
</div>
