<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-147">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-147" class="vocabulary-word-title">employ<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-147" aria-label="定位 employ">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-147" data-mark-label="employ" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="employ：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="employ：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=employ&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 employ" title="在有道查询 employ">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-147</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.雇用；采用</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A firm that plans to hire and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=employ&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>employ</strong></a> new staff should explain any recent lay-off and why it chose to fire workers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">计划招聘并雇用新员工的企业应解释近期裁员，以及为何解雇员工。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Managers cannot simply fire workers after a temporary lay-off and then hire replacements to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=employ&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>employ</strong></a> on lower pay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">经理不能在临时裁员后直接解雇工人，再雇替工并支付更低工资。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before we hire anyone, we must decide whom to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=employ&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>employ</strong></a> permanently and how a lay-off differs from a decision to fire someone.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">招聘前，我们须决定长期雇谁，并区分裁员与解雇。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">employ staff</span> · 雇用员工</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=employment%20contract&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">employment contract</a></span> · 劳动合同</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=job%20security&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">job security</a></span> · 工作保障</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-148">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-148" class="vocabulary-word-title">hire<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-148" aria-label="定位 hire">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-148" data-mark-label="hire" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hire：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hire：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hire" title="在有道查询 hire">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-148</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.雇用：租用自．租赁(业)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A firm that plans to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hire</strong></a> and employ new staff should explain any recent lay-off and why it chose to fire workers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">计划招聘并雇用新员工的企业应解释近期裁员，以及为何解雇员工。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Managers cannot simply fire workers after a temporary lay-off and then <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hire</strong></a> replacements to employ on lower pay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">经理不能在临时裁员后直接解雇工人，再雇替工并支付更低工资。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before we <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hire</strong></a> anyone, we must decide whom to employ permanently and how a lay-off differs from a decision to fire someone.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">招聘前，我们须决定长期雇谁，并区分裁员与解雇。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">hire a designer</span> · 聘请设计师</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=employment%20contract&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">employment contract</a></span> · 劳动合同</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=job%20security&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">job security</a></span> · 工作保障</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-149">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-149" class="vocabulary-word-title">fire<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-149" aria-label="定位 fire">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-149" data-mark-label="fire" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="fire：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="fire：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 fire" title="在有道查询 fire">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-149</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A firm that plans to hire and employ new staff should explain any recent lay-off and why it chose to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fire</strong></a> workers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">计划招聘并雇用新员工的企业应解释近期裁员，以及为何解雇员工。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Managers cannot simply <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fire</strong></a> workers after a temporary lay-off and then hire replacements to employ on lower pay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">经理不能在临时裁员后直接解雇工人，再雇替工并支付更低工资。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before we hire anyone, we must decide whom to employ permanently and how a lay-off differs from a decision to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fire</strong></a> someone.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">招聘前，我们须决定长期雇谁，并区分裁员与解雇。</p></li>
</ol>

</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-150">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-150" class="vocabulary-word-title">lay-off<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-150" aria-label="定位 lay-off">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-150" data-mark-label="lay-off" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="lay-off：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="lay-off：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lay-off&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 lay-off" title="在有道查询 lay-off">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-150</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. (因岗位不足的)解雇，裁员；歇工期</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A firm that plans to hire and employ new staff should explain any recent <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lay-off&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lay-off</strong></a> and why it chose to fire workers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">计划招聘并雇用新员工的企业应解释近期裁员，以及为何解雇员工。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Managers cannot simply fire workers after a temporary <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lay-off&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lay-off</strong></a> and then hire replacements to employ on lower pay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">经理不能在临时裁员后直接解雇工人，再雇替工并支付更低工资。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before we hire anyone, we must decide whom to employ permanently and how a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lay-off&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lay-off</strong></a> differs from a decision to fire someone.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">招聘前，我们须决定长期雇谁，并区分裁员与解雇。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a temporary lay-off</span> · 暂时裁员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=employment%20contract&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">employment contract</a></span> · 劳动合同</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=job%20security&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">job security</a></span> · 工作保障</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-151">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-151" class="vocabulary-word-title">dismiss<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-151" aria-label="定位 dismiss">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-151" data-mark-label="dismiss" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="dismiss：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="dismiss：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dismiss&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 dismiss" title="在有道查询 dismiss">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-151</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 解雇；解散；(法官)驳回</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The handbook explains when managers may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dismiss&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dismiss</strong></a> or discharge staff, sack an executive, accept a decision to resign or allow someone to retire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">手册说明经理何时可解雇员工、开除高管、接受辞职或允许退休。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dismiss&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dismiss</strong></a> workers, discharge trainees or sack managers, the board let volunteers resign or retire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">董事会没有解雇工人、辞退学员或开除经理，而是允许自愿者辞职或退休。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Some employees resign or retire before an investigation can lead the firm to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dismiss&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dismiss</strong></a> them, discharge them from service or sack them.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一些员工在调查导致公司解雇、免职或开除他们之前便辞职或退休。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">dismiss an employee</span> · 解雇员工</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=termination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">termination</a></span> · 雇佣终止</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retirement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">retirement</a></span> · 退休</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-152">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-152" class="vocabulary-word-title">discharge<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-152" aria-label="定位 discharge">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-152" data-mark-label="discharge" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="discharge：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="discharge：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=discharge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 discharge" title="在有道查询 discharge">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-152</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 解雇；n. (船的)卸货</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The handbook explains when managers may dismiss or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=discharge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>discharge</strong></a> staff, sack an executive, accept a decision to resign or allow someone to retire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">手册说明经理何时可解雇员工、开除高管、接受辞职或允许退休。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than dismiss workers, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=discharge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>discharge</strong></a> trainees or sack managers, the board let volunteers resign or retire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">董事会没有解雇工人、辞退学员或开除经理，而是允许自愿者辞职或退休。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Some employees resign or retire before an investigation can lead the firm to dismiss them, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=discharge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>discharge</strong></a> them from service or sack them.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一些员工在调查导致公司解雇、免职或开除他们之前便辞职或退休。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">discharge a worker</span> · 解雇工人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=termination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">termination</a></span> · 雇佣终止</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retirement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">retirement</a></span> · 退休</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-153">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-153" class="vocabulary-word-title">resign<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-153" aria-label="定位 resign">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-153" data-mark-label="resign" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="resign：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="resign：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resign&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 resign" title="在有道查询 resign">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-153</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 辞去，辞职</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The handbook explains when managers may dismiss or discharge staff, sack an executive, accept a decision to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resign&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>resign</strong></a> or allow someone to retire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">手册说明经理何时可解雇员工、开除高管、接受辞职或允许退休。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than dismiss workers, discharge trainees or sack managers, the board let volunteers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resign&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>resign</strong></a> or retire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">董事会没有解雇工人、辞退学员或开除经理，而是允许自愿者辞职或退休。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Some employees <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resign&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>resign</strong></a> or retire before an investigation can lead the firm to dismiss them, discharge them from service or sack them.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一些员工在调查导致公司解雇、免职或开除他们之前便辞职或退休。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">resign from office</span> · 辞职</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=termination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">termination</a></span> · 雇佣终止</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retirement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">retirement</a></span> · 退休</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-154">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-154" class="vocabulary-word-title">sack<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-154" aria-label="定位 sack">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-154" data-mark-label="sack" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sack：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sack：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sack&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sack" title="在有道查询 sack">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-154</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.大口袋，麻布袋；v.开除</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The handbook explains when managers may dismiss or discharge staff, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sack&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sack</strong></a> an executive, accept a decision to resign or allow someone to retire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">手册说明经理何时可解雇员工、开除高管、接受辞职或允许退休。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than dismiss workers, discharge trainees or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sack&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sack</strong></a> managers, the board let volunteers resign or retire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">董事会没有解雇工人、辞退学员或开除经理，而是允许自愿者辞职或退休。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Some employees resign or retire before an investigation can lead the firm to dismiss them, discharge them from service or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sack&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sack</strong></a> them.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一些员工在调查导致公司解雇、免职或开除他们之前便辞职或退休。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">sack a manager</span> · 开除经理</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=termination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">termination</a></span> · 雇佣终止</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retirement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">retirement</a></span> · 退休</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-155">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-155" class="vocabulary-word-title">retire<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-155" aria-label="定位 retire">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-155" data-mark-label="retire" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="retire：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="retire：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 retire" title="在有道查询 retire">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-155</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. (使)退休</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The handbook explains when managers may dismiss or discharge staff, sack an executive, accept a decision to resign or allow someone to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retire</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">手册说明经理何时可解雇员工、开除高管、接受辞职或允许退休。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than dismiss workers, discharge trainees or sack managers, the board let volunteers resign or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retire</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">董事会没有解雇工人、辞退学员或开除经理，而是允许自愿者辞职或退休。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Some employees resign or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retire</strong></a> before an investigation can lead the firm to dismiss them, discharge them from service or sack them.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一些员工在调查导致公司解雇、免职或开除他们之前便辞职或退休。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">retire early</span> · 提前退休</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=termination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">termination</a></span> · 雇佣终止</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retirement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">retirement</a></span> · 退休</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 16.15｜经营结果与后续治理 {#group-16-15}

**串联方式：** 面对结果、失败和损失，识别机会、作出选择，再解决问题、持续发展并改进管理。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-156">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-156" class="vocabulary-word-title">result<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-156" aria-label="定位 result">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-156" data-mark-label="result" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="result：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="result：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=result&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 result" title="在有道查询 result">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-156</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 后果，结果；v. 导致</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The loss was a direct <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=result&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>result</strong></a> of a management failure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这笔损失是管理失败的直接结果。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Business failure can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=result&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>result</strong></a> in the loss of jobs across a whole town.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">经营失败可能导致整个城镇就业岗位流失。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report linked the failure to a loss of trust rather than the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=result&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>result</strong></a> of poor demand.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告将失败与信任流失联系起来，而非归因于需求疲弱。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a direct result</span> · 直接结果</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accountability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accountability</a></span> · 问责</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bankruptcy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bankruptcy</a></span> · 破产</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-157">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report linked the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=failure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>failure</strong></a> to a loss of trust rather than the result of poor demand.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告将失败与信任流失联系起来，而非归因于需求疲弱。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.机会，时机</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A career change offers a chance to choose work with more <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=opportunity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>opportunity</strong></a> for growth.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">转行让人有机会选择更有成长空间的工作。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Choose a course that gives you a chance to make a change and seize a new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=opportunity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>opportunity</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">选择一门能让你有机会改变并抓住新机遇的课程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">This <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=opportunity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>opportunity</strong></a> gives residents a chance to choose how the town should change.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这个机会让居民能选择城镇应该如何改变。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an opportunity to learn</span> · 学习机会</p>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We must develop sustainable transport to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=solve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>solve</strong></a> congestion and improve air quality.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们必须发展可持续交通，以解决拥堵并改善空气质量。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To improve housing, councils should develop sustainable methods that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=solve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>solve</strong></a> problems without wasting resources.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为改善住房，市政机构应制定可持续方法，在不浪费资源的情况下解决问题。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Engineers develop sustainable technologies to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=solve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>solve</strong></a> energy shortages and improve efficiency.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工程师研发可持续技术，以解决能源短缺并提高效率。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">solve a problem</span> · 解决问题</p>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=improve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>improve</strong></a> housing, councils should develop sustainable methods that solve problems without wasting resources.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为改善住房，市政机构应制定可持续方法，在不浪费资源的情况下解决问题。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Engineers develop sustainable technologies to solve energy shortages and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=improve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>improve</strong></a> efficiency.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工程师研发可持续技术，以解决能源短缺并提高效率。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-168">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-168" class="vocabulary-word-title">manage<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-168" aria-label="定位 manage">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-168" data-mark-label="manage" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="manage：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="manage：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=manage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 manage" title="在有道查询 manage">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-168</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 管理；设法做到</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Governments must cooperate to regulate trade, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=manage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>manage</strong></a> disputes and agree on any sanction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">各国政府必须合作监管贸易、处理争端，并就任何制裁达成一致。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An international sanction is easier to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=manage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>manage</strong></a> when agencies cooperate to regulate financial flows.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">当机构合作监管资金流动时，国际制裁更易实施管理。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials who regulate banks must cooperate with those who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=manage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>manage</strong></a> customs before applying a sanction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">监管银行的官员在实施制裁前，必须与管理海关的人员合作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">manage resources</span> · 管理资源</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oversight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">oversight</a></span> · 监督</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=international%20agreement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">international agreement</a></span> · 国际协议</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-169">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-169" class="vocabulary-word-title">regulate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-169" aria-label="定位 regulate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-169" data-mark-label="regulate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="regulate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="regulate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=regulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 regulate" title="在有道查询 regulate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-169</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 管理；调整</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Governments must cooperate to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=regulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>regulate</strong></a> trade, manage disputes and agree on any sanction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">各国政府必须合作监管贸易、处理争端，并就任何制裁达成一致。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An international sanction is easier to manage when agencies cooperate to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=regulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>regulate</strong></a> financial flows.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">当机构合作监管资金流动时，国际制裁更易实施管理。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=regulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>regulate</strong></a> banks must cooperate with those who manage customs before applying a sanction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">监管银行的官员在实施制裁前，必须与管理海关的人员合作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">regulate markets</span> · 监管市场</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oversight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">oversight</a></span> · 监督</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=international%20agreement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">international agreement</a></span> · 国际协议</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-170">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-170" class="vocabulary-word-title">sanction<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-170" aria-label="定位 sanction">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-170" data-mark-label="sanction" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sanction：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sanction：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sanction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sanction" title="在有道查询 sanction">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-170</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 批准；制裁；v. 批准；对……实施制裁</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Governments must cooperate to regulate trade, manage disputes and agree on any <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sanction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sanction</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">各国政府必须合作监管贸易、处理争端，并就任何制裁达成一致。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An international <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sanction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sanction</strong></a> is easier to manage when agencies cooperate to regulate financial flows.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">当机构合作监管资金流动时，国际制裁更易实施管理。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials who regulate banks must cooperate with those who manage customs before applying a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sanction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sanction</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">监管银行的官员在实施制裁前，必须与管理海关的人员合作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an economic sanction</span> · 经济制裁</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oversight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">oversight</a></span> · 监督</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=international%20agreement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">international agreement</a></span> · 国际协议</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="16-171">
<summary><h4 id="word-16-171" class="vocabulary-word-title">cooperate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-16-171" aria-label="定位 cooperate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="16-171" data-mark-label="cooperate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="cooperate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="cooperate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cooperate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 cooperate" title="在有道查询 cooperate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">16-171</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 合作，协作</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Governments must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cooperate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cooperate</strong></a> to regulate trade, manage disputes and agree on any sanction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">各国政府必须合作监管贸易、处理争端，并就任何制裁达成一致。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An international sanction is easier to manage when agencies <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cooperate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cooperate</strong></a> to regulate financial flows.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">当机构合作监管资金流动时，国际制裁更易实施管理。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials who regulate banks must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cooperate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cooperate</strong></a> with those who manage customs before applying a sanction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">监管银行的官员在实施制裁前，必须与管理海关的人员合作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">cooperate closely</span> · 紧密合作</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oversight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">oversight</a></span> · 监督</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=international%20agreement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">international agreement</a></span> · 国际协议</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 本章 Language Takeaway

优先尝试主动说出的三个表达：

- **generate revenue**：创造营业收入；generate 为补充词。
- **create job opportunities**：创造就业机会；create 为补充词。
- **sustainable development**：可持续发展；development 来自 develop。

### 串学示范｜自拟短文

A company invests money to develop products and sell goods. Its revenue must cover costs such as wages, materials and rent. When demand grows, the firm may hire more workers; during a recession, it may reduce spending. Sustainable development requires attention to both opportunities and risks.

**中文理解：** 企业投入资金开发产品并销售商品。营业收入需要覆盖工资、材料和租金等成本。需求增长时，企业可能雇用更多员工；经济衰退时，企业可能减少支出。可持续发展需要同时关注机会和风险。
